# Review and improve PROGRESS.md with theming integration

Continuing work from thread T-7b92a860-a2f7-4397-985c-73b2fa3e9582. When you lack specific information you can use read_thread to get it.

@docs/PROGRESS.md @docs/CODEBASE_REVIEW.md, @docs/THEMING_SYSTEM.md @docs/CODEBASE_REVIEW.md @EDITOR_UI_REFERENCE.md @src/main.rs @TODO.md

I reviewed and rewrote EDITOR_UI_REFERENCE.md with comprehensive coverage of text editor geometry, viewport calculations, soft wrapping, cursor styles, and coordinate transformations. This serves as the target feature set.

I then reviewed the current Elm-style architecture in main.rs and created CODEBASE_REVIEW.md with these key recommendations:
- Keep the Elm-style Msg → update → Model → view pattern (it's solid)
- Split the monolithic Model into Document/EditorState/UiState
- Nest the flat Msg enum by domain: EditorMsg, DocumentMsg, UiMsg, AppMsg
- Expand Cmd beyond just Redraw to support async operations (SaveFile, LoadFile, SpawnTask)
- Add Selection and multi-cursor support
- The architecture should have an AppModel containing: documents HashMap, editors HashMap, ui state, debug state

I also added a section on Performance Monitoring integration using perf-monitor-rs:
- DebugState as a separate domain in AppModel
- DebugMsg for toggling overlay and receiving stats
- Cmd::StartPerfSampler for background thread sampling
- Compile-gated with #[cfg(feature = "perf")]

I then designed a theming system in docs/THEMING_SYSTEM.md:
- YAML format for human-editable theme files
- Hierarchical naming: ui.editor.background.normal, syntax.keyword.foreground
- State modifiers: normal, hover, active, focused, disabled
- Theme struct lives in AppModel alongside documents/editors/ui
- ThemeMsg for load/reload, Cmd::LoadTheme for async loading
- Example Fleet Dark theme with actual colors

Key constraints I mentioned:
- Target audience is desktop software (Rust, Go, C++), not web
- Monospace fonts only
- Left-to-right text only (no RTL/BiDi support needed)
- No support for non-Latin languages or Chinese input methods
- preferredColumn is a column integer (not pixels) for vertical nav memory

review and improve the @docs/PROGRESS.md  plan to ensure it is consistant with our suggestions in @docs/CODEBASE_REVIEW.md, also add a section/phasee about integrating the @docs/THEMING_SYSTEM.md  as part of this refactor

Blocking I/O in update()\n  AppMsg::SaveFile => {\n      std::fs::write(path, content)?;\n      // ...\n  }\n\n  // After: Return command, handle result\n  AppMsg::SaveFile => {\n      app.ui.is_saving = true;\n      app.ui.status_message = \"Saving...\".to_string();\n      Some(Cmd::SaveFile { path, content })\n  }\n\n  AppMsg::SaveCompleted(result) => {\n      app.ui.is_saving = AppMsg::LoadFile(path) => {\n      app.ui.is_loading = true;\n      app.ui.status_message = \"Loading...\".to_string();\n      Some(Cmd::LoadFile { path })\n  }\n\n  AppMsg::FileLoaded { path, result } => {\n      app.ui.is_loading = false;\n      match result {\n          Ok(content) => {\n              app.document.buffer = Rope::from(content);\n              app.document.file_path = Some(path);\n              app.document.is_modified = false;\n              app.document.undo_stack.clear();\n              app.document.redo_stack.clear();\n              app.editor.cursor = Cursor::default();\n              app.ui.status_message = format!(\"Loaded: {}\", path);\n          }\n          Err(e) => {\n              app.ui.status_message = format!(\"Error: {}\", e);\n          }\n      }\n      Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n  }\n  ```\n\n- [ ] **3.6** Create command processor\n\n  ```rust\n  fn process_cmd(cmd: Cmd, tx: Sender<Msg>) {\n      match cmd {\n          Cmd::Redraw => {\n EditOperation};\n10: pub use editor::{Cursor, EditorState, Viewport};\n11: pub use ui::UiState;\n12: \n13: use std::path::PathBuf;\n14: \n15: /// Layout constant - width of line number gutter in characters\n16: pub const LINE_NUMBER_GUTTER_CHARS: usize = 6;\n17: \n18: /// The complete application model\n19: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n20: pub struct AppModel {\n21:     /// Document state (text buffer, file, undo/redo)\n22:     pub document: Document,\n23:     /// Editor state (cursor, viewport, scroll settings)\n24:     pub editor: EditorState,\n25:     /// UI state (status bar, cursor blink)\n26:     pub ui: UiState,\n27:     /// Window dimensions\n28:     pub window_size: (u32, u32),\n29:     /// Line height in pixels\n30:     pub line_height: usize,\n31:     /// Character width in pixels (monospace)\n32:     pub char_width: f32,\n33: }\n34: \n35: impl AppModel {\n36:     /// Create a new application model with the given window size\n37:     pub fn new(window_width: u32, window_height: u32, file_path: Option<PathBuf>) -> Self {\n38:         let line_height = 20;\n39:         let char_width: f32 = 10.0; // Will be corrected by renderer with actual font metrics\n40: \n41:         // Load file if provided, otherwise use demo text\n42:         let (document, status_message) = match file_path {\n43:             Some(path) => match Document::from_file(path.clone()) {\n44:                 Ok(doc) => {\n45:                     let msg = format!(\"Loaded: {}\", path.display());\n46:                     (doc, msg)\n47:                 }\n48:                 Err(e) => {\n49:                     let msg = format!(\"Error loading {}: {}\", path.display(), e);\n50:                     (Document::new(), msg)\n51:                 }\n52:             },\n53:             None => {\n54:                 let doc = Document::with_text(\n55:                     \"Hello, World!\\nThis is a text editor built in Rust.\\nUsing Elm architecture!\\n\\nStart typing to edit.\\n\"\n56:                 );\n57:                 (doc, \"New file\".to_string())\n58:             }\n59:         };\n60: \n61:         // Calculate viewport dimensions\n62:         let text_start_x = (char_width * LINE_NUMBER_GUTTER_CHARS as f32).round();\n63:         let visible_columns = ((window_width as f32 - text_start_x) / char_width).floor() as usize;\n64:         let visible_lines = (window_height as usize) / line_height;\n65: \n66:         Self {\n67:             document,\n68:             editor: EditorState::with_viewport(visible_lines, visible_columns),\n69:             ui: UiState::with_status(status_message),\n70:             window_size: (window_width, window_height),\n71:             line_height,\n72:             char_width,\n73:         }\n74:     }\n75: \n76:     /// Update viewport dimensions after window resize\n77:     pub fn resize(&mut self, width: u32, height: u32) {\n78:         self.window_size = (width, height);\n79: \n80:         let text_start_x = (self.char_width * LINE_NUMBER_GUTTER_CHARS as f32).round();\n81:         let visible_columns = ((width as f32 - text_start_x) / self.char_width).floor() as usize;\n82:         let visible_lines = (height as usize) / self.line_height;\n83: \n84:         self.editor.resize_viewport(visible_lines, visible_columns);\n85:     }\n86: \n87:     /// Update char_width from actual font metrics\n88:     pub fn set_char_width(&mut self, char_width: f32) {\n89:         self.char_width = char_width;\n90: \n91:         // Recalculate visible columns with new char width\n92:         let text_start_x = (char_width * LINE_NUMBER_GUTTER_CHARS as f32).round();\n93:         let visible_columns = ((self.window_size.0 as f32 - text_start_x) / char_width).floor() as usize;\n94: \n95:         self.editor.viewport.visible_columns = visible_columns;\n96:     }\n97: \n98:     // Convenience methods that delegate to sub-models\n99: \n100:     /// Get the buffer offset for the current cursor position\n101:     pub fn cursor_buffer_position(&self) -> usize {\n102:         self.editor.cursor_offset(&self.document)\n103:     }\n104: \n105:     /// Set cursor position from buffer offset\n106:     pub fn set_cursor_from_position(&mut self, pos: usize) {\n107:         self.editor.set_cursor_from_offset(&self.document, pos);\n108:     }\n109: \n110:     /// Get the current line length\n111:     pub fn current_line_length(&self) -> usize {\n112:         self.editor.current_line_length(&self.document)\n113:     }\n114: \n115:     /// Ensure cursor is visible in viewport\n116:     pub fn ensure_cursor_visible(&mut self) {\n117:         self.editor.ensure_cursor_visible(&self.document);\n118:     }\n119: \n120:     /// Reset cursor blink timer\n121:     pub fn reset_cursor_blink(&mut self) {\n122:         self.ui.reset_cursor_blink();\n123:     }\n124: \n125:     /// Get a line from the document\n126:     pub fn get_line(&self, line_idx: usize) -> Option<String> {\n127:         self.document.get_line(line_idx)\n128:     }\n129: \n130:     /// Get line length\n131:     pub fn line_length(&self, line_idx: usize) -> usize {\n132:         self.document.line_length(line_idx)\n133:     }\n134: \n135:     /// Get first non-whitespace column on current line\n136:     pub fn first_non_whitespace_column(&self) -> usize {\n137:         self.document.first_non_whitespace_column(self.editor.cursor.line)\n138:     }\n139: \n140:     /// Get last non-whitespace column on current line\n141:     pub fn last_non_whitespace_column(&self) -> usize {\n142:         self.document.last_non_whitespace_column(self.editor.cursor.line)\n143:     }\n144: }",

}

  "content": "1: //! Message types for the Elm-style architecture\n2: //!\n3: //! All state changes flow through these message types.\n4: \n5: use std::path::PathBuf;\n6: \n7: /// Direction for cursor movement\n8: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\n9: pub enum Direction {\n10:     Up,\n11:     Down,\n12:     Left,\n13:     Right,\n14: }\n15: \n16: /// Editor-specific messages (cursor movement, viewport scrolling)\n17: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n18: pub enum EditorMsg {\n19:     /// Move cursor in a direction\n20:     MoveCursor(Direction),\n21:     /// Move cursor to start of line (Home key)\n22:     MoveCursorLineStart,\n23:     /// Move cursor to end of line (End key)\n24:     MoveCursorLineEnd,\n25:     /// Move cursor to start of document (Ctrl+Home)\n26:     MoveCursorDocumentStart,\n27:     /// Move cursor to end of document (Ctrl+End)\n28:     MoveCursorDocumentEnd,\n29:     /// Move cursor by word (Option+Left/Right on Mac)\n30:     MoveCursorWord(Direction),\n31:     /// Page up\n32:     PageUp,\n33:     /// Page down\n34:     PageDown,\n35:     /// Set cursor to specific position (from mouse click)\n36:     SetCursorPosition { line: usize, column: usize },\n37:     /// Scroll viewport vertically (positive = down, negative = up)\n38:     Scroll(i32),\n39:     /// Scroll viewport horizontally (positive = right, negative = left)\n40:     ScrollHorizontal(i32),\n41: }\n42: \n43: /// Document-specific messages (text editing, undo/redo)\n44: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n45: pub enum DocumentMsg {\n46:     /// Insert a character at cursor\n47:     InsertChar(char),\n48:     /// Insert a newline at cursor\n49:     InsertNewline,\n50:     /// Delete character before cursor (Backspace)\n51:     DeleteBackward,\n52:     /// Delete character at cursor (Delete)\n53:     DeleteForward,\n54:     /// Undo last edit\n55:     Undo,\n56:     /// Redo last undone edit\n57:     Redo,\n58: }\n59: \n60: /// UI-specific messages (status bar, cursor blink)\n61: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n62: pub enum UiMsg {\n63:     /// Set status bar message\n64:     SetStatus(String),\n65:     /// Toggle cursor blink state\n66:     BlinkCursor,\n67: }\n68: \n69: /// Application-level messages (file operations, window events)\n70: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n71: pub enum AppMsg {\n72:     /// Window resized\n73:     Resize(u32, u32),\n74:     /// Save current file\n75:     SaveFile,\n76:     /// Load a file\n77:     LoadFile(PathBuf),\n78:     /// Create a new file\n79:     NewFile,\n80:     /// File save completed (async result)\n81:     SaveCompleted(Result<(), String>),\n82:     /// File load completed (async result)\n83:     FileLoaded {\n84:         path: PathBuf,\n85:         result: Result<String, String>,\n86:     },\n87:     /// Quit the application\n88:     Quit,\n89: }\n90: \n91: /// Top-level message type\n92: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n93: pub enum Msg {\n94:     /// Editor messages (cursor, viewport)\n95:     Editor(EditorMsg),\n96:     /// Document messages (text editing)\n97:     Document(DocumentMsg),\n98:     /// UI messages (status, animation)\n99:     Ui(UiMsg),\n100:     /// App messages (file I/O, window)\n101:     App(AppMsg),\n102: }\n103: \n104: // Convenience constructors for common messages\n105: impl Msg {\n106:     /// Create a cursor movement message\n107:     pub fn move_cursor(direction: Direction) -> Self {\n108:         Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::MoveCursor(direction))\n109:     }\n110: \n111:     /// Create an insert character message\n112:     pub fn insert_char(ch: char) -> Self {\n113:         Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::InsertChar(ch))\n114:     }\n115: \n116:     /// Create a resize message\n117:     pub fn resize(width: u32, height: u32) -> Self {\n118:         Msg::App(AppMsg::Resize(width, height))\n119:     }\n120: }",

}

{
  "content": "1: //! Command types for the Elm-style architecture\n2: //!\n3: //! Commands represent side effects that should be performed after an update.\n4: \n5: use std::path::PathBuf;\n6: \n7: /// Commands returned by update functions\n8: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n9: pub enum Cmd {\n10:     /// No command - do nothing\n11:     None,\n12:     /// Request a redraw of the UI\n13:     Redraw,\n14:     /// Save file asynchronously\n15:     SaveFile {\n16:         path: PathBuf,\n17:         content: String,\n18:     },\n19:     /// Load file asynchronously\n20:     LoadFile {\n21:         path: PathBuf,\n22:     },\n23:     /// Execute multiple commands\n24:     Batch(Vec<Cmd>),\n25: }\n26: \n27: impl Cmd {\n28:     /// Create a batch of commands\n29:     pub fn batch(cmds: Vec<Cmd>) -> Self {\n30:         Cmd::Batch(cmds)\n31:     }\n32: \n33:     /// Check if this command requires a redraw\n34:     pub fn needs_redraw(&self) -> bool {\n35:         match self {\n36:             Cmd::None => false,\n37:             Cmd::Redraw => true,\n38:             Cmd::SaveFile { .. } => true, // Show \"Saving...\" status\n39:             Cmd::LoadFile { .. } => true, // Show \"Loading...\" status\n40:             Cmd::Batch(cmds) => cmds.iter().any(|c| c.needs_redraw()),\n41:         }\n42:     }\n43: \n44:     /// Convert Option<Cmd> with None to Cmd::None\n45:     pub fn from_option(opt: Option<Cmd>) -> Self {\n46:         opt.unwrap_or(Cmd::None)\n47:     }\n48: }\n49: \n50: impl Default for Cmd {\n51:     fn default() -> Self {\n52:         Cmd::None\n53:     }\n54: }\n55: \n56: // Allow converting Option<Cmd> to Cmd\n57: impl From<Option<Cmd>> for Cmd {\n58:     fn from(opt: Option<Cmd>) -> Self {\n59:         opt.unwrap_or(Cmd::None)\n60:     }\n61: }",
  "absolutePath": "~/code/rust-editor/src/commands.rs"
}

  "content": "1: //! Document model - represents the text buffer and file state\n2: \n3: use ropey::Rope;\n4: use std::path::PathBuf;\n5: \n6: use super::editor::Cursor;\n7: \n8: /// Represents an edit operation for undo/redo functionality\n9: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n10: pub enum EditOperation {\n11:     Insert {\n12:         position: usize,\n13:         text: String,\n14:         cursor_before: Cursor,\n15:         cursor_after: Cursor,\n16:     },\n17:     Delete {\n18:         position: usize,\n19:         text: String,\n20:         cursor_before: Cursor,\n21:         cursor_after: Cursor,\n22:     },\n23: }\n24: \n25: /// Document state - the text buffer and associated file metadata\n26: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n27: pub struct Document {\n28:     /// The text buffer\n29:     pub buffer: Rope,\n30:     /// Path to the file on disk (None for new/unsaved files)\n31:     pub file_path: Option<PathBuf>,\n32:     /// Whether the buffer has unsaved changes\n33:     pub is_modified: bool,\n34:     /// Undo stack\n35:     pub undo_stack: Vec<EditOperation>,\n36:     /// Redo stack\n37:     pub redo_stack: Vec<EditOperation>,\n38: }\n39: \n40: impl Document {\n41:     /// Create a new empty document\n42:     pub fn new() -> Self {\n43:         Self {\n44:             buffer: Rope::from(\"\"),\n45:             file_path: None,\n46:             is_modified: false,\n47:             undo_stack: Vec::new(),\n48:             redo_stack: Vec::new(),\n49:         }\n50:     }\n51: \n52:     /// Create a document with initial text\n53:     pub fn with_text(text: &str) -> Self {\n54:         Self {\n55:             buffer: Rope::from(text),\n56:             file_path: None,\n57:             is_modified: false,\n58:             undo_stack: Vec::new(),\n59:             redo_stack: Vec::new(),\n60:         }\n61:     }\n62: \n63:     /// Load a document from a file path\n64:     pub fn from_file(path: PathBuf) -> Result<Self, std::io::Error> {\n65:         let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&path)?;\n66:         Ok(Self {\n67:             buffer: Rope::from(content),\n68:             file_path: Some(path),\n69:             is_modified: false,\n70:             undo_stack: Vec::new(),\n71:             redo_stack: Vec::new(),\n72:         })\n73:     }\n74: \n75:     /// Get the number of lines in the document\n76:     pub fn line_count(&self) -> usize {\n77:         self.buffer.len_lines()\n78:     }\n79: \n80:     /// Get a line by index\n81:     pub fn get_line(&self, line_idx: usize) -> Option<String> {\n82:         if line_idx < self.buffer.len_lines() {\n83:             let line = self.buffer.line(line_idx);\n84:             Some(line.to_string())\n85:         } else {\n86:             None\n87:         }\n88:     }\n89: \n90:     /// Get the length of a line (excluding newline character)\n91:     pub fn line_length(&self, line_idx: usize) -> usize {\n92:         if line_idx < self.buffer.len_lines() {\n93:             let line = self.buffer.line(line_idx);\n94:             line.len_chars().saturating_sub(\n95:                 if line.len_chars() > 0 && line.chars().last() == Some('\\n') {\n96:                     1\n97:                 } else {\n98:                     0\n99:                 },\n100:             )\n101:         } else {\n102:             0\n103:         }\n104:     }\n105: \n106:     /// Convert a (line, column) position to a buffer offset\n107:     pub fn cursor_to_offset(&self, line: usize, column: usize) -> usize {\n108:         let mut pos = 0;\n109:         for i in 0..line {\n110:             if i < self.buffer.len_lines() {\n111:                 pos += self.buffer.line(i).len_chars();\n112:             }\n113:         }\n114:         pos + column.min(self.line_length(line))\n115:     }\n116: \n117:     /// Convert a buffer offset to (line, column) position\n118:     pub fn offset_to_cursor(&self, offset: usize) -> (usize, usize) {\n119:         let mut remaining = offset;\n120:         for line_idx in 0..self.buffer.len_lines() {\n121:             let line = self.buffer.line(line_idx);\n122:             let line_len = line.len_chars();\n123:             if remaining < line_len {\n124:                 return (line_idx, remaining);\n125:             }\n126:             remaining -= line_len;\n127:         }\n128:         // Past end - return end of document\n129:         let last_line = self.buffer.len_lines().saturating_sub(1);\n130:         (last_line, self.line_length(last_line))\n131:     }\n132: \n133:     /// Get the column of the first non-whitespace character on a line\n134:     pub fn first_non_whitespace_column(&self, line_idx: usize) -> usize {\n135:         if line_idx >= self.buffer.len_lines() {\n136:             return 0;\n137:         }\n138:         let line = self.buffer.line(line_idx);\n139:         line.chars()\n140:             .take_while(|c| c.is_whitespace() && *c != '\\n')\n141:             .count()\n142:     }\n143: \n144:     /// Get the column after the last non-whitespace character on a line\n145:     pub fn last_non_whitespace_column(&self, line_idx: usize) -> usize {\n146:         if line_idx >= self.buffer.len_lines() {\n147:             return 0;\n148:         }\n149:         let line = self.buffer.line(line_idx);\n150:         let line_str: String = line.chars().collect();\n151:         let trimmed = line_str.trim_end_matches(|c: char| c.is_whitespace());\n152:         trimmed.len()\n153:     }\n154: \n155:     /// Push an edit operation onto the undo stack and clear redo stack\n156:     pub fn push_edit(&mut self, op: EditOperation) {\n157:         self.undo_stack.push(op);\n158:         self.redo_stack.clear();\n159:         self.is_modified = true;\n160:     }\n161: }\n162: \n163: impl Default for Document {\n164:     fn default() -> Self {\n165:         Self::new()\n166:     }\n167: }",

  "content": "1: //! Editor state - cursor, viewport, and view-specific state\n2: \n3: use super::document::Document;\n4: \n5: /// Cursor position in the document\n6: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]\n7: pub struct Cursor {\n8:     /// Line number (0-indexed)\n9:     pub line: usize,\n10:     /// Column number (0-indexed)\n11:     pub column: usize,\n12:     /// Desired column for vertical movement (preserves position when moving through short lines)\n13:     pub desired_column: Option<usize>,\n14: }\n15: \n16: impl Cursor {\n17:     /// Create a new cursor at position (0, 0)\n18:     pub fn new() -> Self {\n19:         Self::default()\n20:     }\n21: \n22:     /// Create a cursor at a specific position\n23:     pub fn at(line: usize, column: usize) -> Self {\n24:         Self {\n25:             line,\n26:             column,\n27:             desired_column: None,\n28:         }\n29:     }\n30: \n31:     /// Reset the desired column (called after horizontal movement)\n32:     pub fn clear_desired_column(&mut self) {\n33:         self.desired_column = None;\n34:     }\n35: \n36:     /// Set the desired column (called before vertical movement if not set)\n37:     pub fn remember_column(&mut self) {\n38:         if self.desired_column.is_none() {\n39:             self.desired_column = Some(self.column);\n40:         }\n41:     }\n42: }\n43: \n44: /// Viewport state - what portion of the document is visible\n45: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n46: pub struct Viewport {\n47:     /// First visible line (0-indexed)\n48:     pub top_line: usize,\n49:     /// First visible column (for horizontal scrolling)\n50:     pub left_column: usize,\n51:     /// Number of lines that fit in the viewport\n52:     pub visible_lines: usize,\n53:     /// Number of columns that fit in the viewport\n54:     pub visible_columns: usize,\n55: }\n56: \n57: impl Viewport {\n58:     /// Create a new viewport with the given dimensions\n59:     pub fn new(visible_lines: usize, visible_columns: usize) -> Self {\n60:         Self {\n61:             top_line: 0,\n62:             left_column: 0,\n63:             visible_lines,\n64:             visible_columns,\n65:         }\n66:     }\n67: }\n68: \n69: impl Default for Viewport {\n70:     fn default() -> Self {\n71:         Self::new(25, 80)\n72:     }\n73: }\n74: \n75: /// Editor state - view-specific state for editing a document\n76: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n77: pub struct EditorState {\n78:     /// Current cursor position\n79:     pub cursor: Cursor,\n80:     /// Viewport showing which portion of the document is visible\n81:     pub viewport: Viewport,\n82:     /// Number of lines of padding to maintain above/below cursor when scrolling\n83:     pub scroll_padding: usize,\n84: }\n85: \n86: impl EditorState {\n87:     /// Create a new editor state with default settings\n88:     pub fn new() -> Self {\n89:         Self {\n90:             cursor: Cursor::new(),\n91:             viewport: Viewport::default(),\n92:             scroll_padding: 1, // JetBrains-style default\n93:         }\n94:     }\n95: \n96:     /// Create an editor state with specific viewport dimensions\n97:     pub fn with_viewport(visible_lines: usize, visible_columns: usize) -> Self {\n98:         Self {\n99:             cursor: Cursor::new(),\n100:             viewport: Viewport::new(visible_lines, visible_columns),\n101:             scroll_padding: 1,\n102:         }\n103:     }\n104: \n105:     /// Update viewport dimensions (e.g., on window resize)\n106:     pub fn resize_viewport(&mut self, visible_lines: usize, visible_columns: usize) {\n107:         self.viewport.visible_lines = visible_lines;\n108:         self.viewport.visible_columns = visible_columns;\n109:     }\n110: \n111:     /// Ensure the cursor is visible within the viewport with padding\n112:     pub fn ensure_cursor_visible(&mut self, document: &Document) {\n113:         let padding = self.scroll_padding;\n114:         let total_lines = document.line_count();\n115: \n116:         // Vertical scrolling\n117:         if total_lines > self.viewport.visible_lines {\n118:             let top_boundary = self.viewport.top_line + padding;\n119:             let bottom_boundary =\n120:                 self.viewport.top_line + self.viewport.visible_lines - padding - 1;\n121: \n122:             // Snap viewport to show cursor with padding\n123:             if self.cursor.line < top_boundary {\n124:                 self.viewport.top_line = self.cursor.line.saturating_sub(padding);\n125:             } else if self.cursor.line > bottom_boundary {\n126:                 let desired_top = self.cursor.line + padding + 1;\n127:                 self.viewport.top_line = desired_top.saturating_sub(self.viewport.visible_lines);\n128:             }\n129: \n130:             // Clamp to valid range\n131:             let max_top = total_lines.saturating_sub(self.viewport.visible_lines);\n132:             self.viewport.top_line = self.viewport.top_line.min(max_top);\n133:         } else {\n134:             self.viewport.top_line = 0;\n135:         }\n136: \n137:         // Horizontal scrolling (always check, independent of vertical)\n138:         const HORIZONTAL_MARGIN: usize = 4;\n139:         let left_safe = self.viewport.left_column + HORIZONTAL_MARGIN;\n140:         let right_safe = self.viewport.left_column + self.viewport.visible_columns - HORIZONTAL_MARGIN;\n141: \n142:         if self.cursor.column < left_safe {\n143:             // Scroll left: put cursor exactly at left safe boundary\n144:             self.viewport.left_column = self.cursor.column.saturating_sub(HORIZONTAL_MARGIN);\n145:         } else if self.cursor.column >= right_safe {\n146:             // Scroll right: put cursor exactly at right safe boundary\n147:             self.viewport.left_column =\n148:                 self.cursor.column + HORIZONTAL_MARGIN + 1 - self.viewport.visible_columns;\n149:         }\n150:     }\n151: \n152:     /// Set cursor position from buffer offset\n153:     pub fn set_cursor_from_offset(&mut self, document: &Document, offset: usize) {\n154:         let (line, column) = document.offset_to_cursor(offset);\n155:         self.cursor.line = line;\n156:         self.cursor.column = column;\n157:         self.cursor.desired_column = None;\n158:     }\n159: \n160:     /// Get buffer offset from current cursor position\n161:     pub fn cursor_offset(&self, document: &Document) -> usize {\n162:         document.cursor_to_offset(self.cursor.line, self.cursor.column)\n163:     }\n164: \n165:     /// Get the length of the current line\n166:     pub fn current_line_length(&self, document: &Document) -> usize {\n167:         document.line_length(self.cursor.line)\n168:     }\n169: }\n170: \n171: impl Default for EditorState {\n172:     fn default() -> Self {\n173:         Self::new()\n174:     }\n175: }",

struct UiState {\n8:     /// Message displayed in the status bar\n9:     pub status_message: String,\n10:     /// Whether the cursor is currently visible (for blinking)\n11:     pub cursor_visible: bool,\n12:     /// Timestamp of last cursor blink state change\n13:     pub last_cursor_blink: Instant,\n14:     /// Whether a file is currently being loaded\n15:     pub is_loading: bool,\n16:     /// Whether a file is currently being saved\n17:     pub is_saving: bool,\n18: }\n19: \n20: impl UiState {\n21:     /// Create a new UI state with default settings\n22:     pub fn new() -> Self {\n23:         Self {\n24:             status_message: String::new(),\n25:             cursor_visible: true,\n26:             last_cursor_blink: Instant::now(),\n27:             is_loading: false,\n28:             is_saving: false,\n29:         }\n30:     }\n31: \n32:     /// Create a UI state with an initial status message\n33:     pub fn with_status(message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n34:         Self {\n35:             status_message: message.into(),\n36:             cursor_visible: true,\n37:             last_cursor_blink: Instant::now(),\n38:             is_loading: false,\n39:             is_saving: false,\n40:         }\n41:     }\n42: \n43:     /// Reset cursor blink timer (call after user input)\n44:     pub fn reset_cursor_blink(&mut self) {\n45:         self.cursor_visible = true;\n46:         self.last_cursor_blink = Instant::now();\n47:     }\n48: \n49:     /// Update cursor blink state based on elapsed time\n50:     /// Returns true if the state changed (needs redraw)\n51:     pub fn update_cursor_blink(&mut self, blink_interval: Duration) -> bool {\n52:         if self.last_cursor_blink.elapsed() >= blink_interval {\n53:             self.cursor_visible = !self.cursor_visible;\n54:             self.last_cursor_blink = Instant::now();\n55:             true\n56:         } else {\n57:             false\n58:         }\n59:     }\n60: \n61:     /// Set the status message\n62:     pub fn set_status(&mut self, message: impl Into<String>) {\n63:         self.status_message = message.into();\n64:     }\n65: \n66:     /// Check if the UI is busy (loading or saving)\n67:     pub fn is_busy(&self) -> bool {\n68:         self.is_loading || self.is_saving\n69:     }\n70: }\n71: \n72: impl Default for UiState {\n73:     fn default() -> Self {\n74: This module contains all the state types following the Elm Architecture pattern.\n4: \n5: pub mod document;\n6: pub mod editor;\n7: pub mod ui;\n8: \n9: pub use document::{Document, EditOperation};\n10: pub use editor::{Cursor, EditorState, Position, Selection, Viewport};\n11: pub use ui::UiState;\n12: \n13: use std::path::PathBuf;\n14: use crate::theme::Theme;\n15: \n16: /// Layout constant - width of line number gutter in characters\n17: pub const LINE_NUMBER_GUTTER_CHARS: usize = 6;\n18: \n19: /// The complete application model\n20: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n21: pub struct AppModel {\n22:     /// Document state (text buffer, file, undo/redo)\n23:     pub document: Document,\n24:     /// Editor state (cursor, viewport, scroll settings)\n25:     pub editor: EditorState,\n26:     /// UI state (status bar, cursor blink)\n27:     pub ui: UiState,\n28:     /// Theme for colors and styling\n29:     pub theme: Theme,\n30:     /// Window dimensions\n31:     pub window_size: (u32, u32),\n32:     /// Line height in pixels\n33:     pub line_height: usize,\n34:     /// Character width in pixels (monospace)\n35:     pub char_width: f32,\n36: }\n37: \n38: impl AppModel {\n39:     /// Create a new application model with the given window size\n40:     pub fn new(window_width: u32, window_height: u32, file_path: Option<PathBuf>) -> Self {\n41:         let line_height = 20;\n42:         let char_width: f32 = 10.0; // Will be corrected by renderer with actual font metrics\n43: \n44:         // Load file if provided, otherwise use demo text\n45:         let (document, status_message) = match file_path {\n46:             Some(path) => match Document::from_file(path.clone()) {\n47:                 Ok(doc) => {\n48:                     let msg = format!(\"Loaded: {}\", path.display());\n49:                     (doc, msg)\n50:                 }\n51:                 Err(e) => {\n52:                     let msg = format!(\"Error loading {}: {}\", path.display(), e);\n53:                     (Document::new(), msg)\n54:                 }\n55:             },\n56:             None => {\n57:                 let doc = Document::with_text(\n58:                     \"Hello, World!\\nThis is a text editor built in Rust.\\nUsing Elm architecture!\\n\\nStart typing to edit.\\n\"\n59:                 );\n60:                 (doc, \"New file\".to_string())\n61:             }\n62:         };\n63: \n64:         // Calculate viewport dimensions\n65:         let text_start_x = (char_width * LINE_NUMBER_GUTTER_CHARS as f32).round();\n66:         let visible_columns = ((window_width as f32 - text_start_x) / char_width).floor() as usize;\n67:         let visible_lines = (window_height as usize) / line_height;\n68: \n69:         Self {\n70:             document,\n71:             editor: EditorState::with_viewport(visible_lines, visible_columns),\n72:             ui: UiState::with_status(status_message),\n73:             theme: Theme::default(),\n74:             window_size: (window_width, window_height),\n75:             line_height,\n76:             char_width,\n77:         }\n78:     }\n79: \n80:     /// Update viewport dimensions after window resize\n81:     pub fn resize(&mut self, width: u32, height: u32) {\n82:         self.window_size = (width, height);\n83: \n84:         let text_start_x = (self.char_width * LINE_NUMBER_GUTTER_CHARS as f32).round();\n85:         let visible_columns = ((width as f32 - text_start_x) / self.char_width).floor() as usize;\n86:         let visible_lines = (height as usize) / self.line_height;\n87: \n88:         self.editor.resize_viewport(visible_lines, visible_columns);\n89:     }\n90: \n91:     /// Update char_width from actual font metrics\n92:     pub fn set_char_width(&mut self, char_width: f32) {\n93:         self.char_width = char_width;\n94: \n95:         // Recalculate visible columns with new char width\n96:         let text_start_x = (char_width * LINE_NUMBER_GUTTER_CHARS as f32).round();\n97:         let visible_columns = ((self.window_size.0 as f32 - text_start_x) / char_width).floor() as usize;\n98: \n99:         self.editor.viewport.visible_columns = visible_columns;\n100:     }\n101: \n102:     // Convenience methods that delegate to sub-models\n103: \n104:     /// Get the buffer offset for the current cursor position\n105:     pub fn cursor_buffer_position(&self) -> usize {\n106:         self.editor.cursor_offset(&self.document)\n107:     }\n108: \n109:     /// Set cursor position from buffer length\n115:     pub fn current_line_length(&self) -> usize {\n116:         self.editor.current_line_length(&self.document)\n117:     }\n118: \n119:     /// Ensure cursor is visible in viewport\n120:   pub fn clear_selection(&mut self) {\n236:         let pos = self.cursors[0].to_position();\n237:         self.selections[0] = Selection::new(pos);\n238:     }\n239: \n240:     /// Update viewport Web-specific concerns (DOM, CSS) are\n8: > mentioned only where relevant for conceptual understanding.\n9: \n10: ---\n11: \n12: ## Table of Contents\n13: \n14: 1. [Foundational Concepts: The Scrollable Region](#chapter-1-foundational-concepts-the-scrollable-region)\n15: 2. [Structural Hierarchy: Tabs, Splits, and Editor Groups](#chapter-2-structural-hierarchy-tabs-splits-and-editor-groups)\n16: 3. [The Gutter System](#chapter-3-the-gutter-system)\n17: 4. [The Complete Render Pipeline](#chapter-9-the-complete-render-pipeline)\n23: 10. [Edge Cases and Special Considerations](#chapter-10-edge-cases-and-special-considerations)\n24: 11. [Naming Conventions and Terminology](#chapter-11-naming-conventions-and-terminology)\n25: 12. [Cursor Styles: Pipe, Block, and Underline](#chapter-12-cursor-styles-pipe-block-and-underline)\n26: \n27: **Appendices:**\n28: \n29: - [A: Event Flow Example](#appendix-a-event-flow-example)\n30: - [B: Coordinate Transformation Functions](#appendix-b-coordinate-transformation-functions)\n31: - [C: Binary Search Helpers](#appendix-c-binary-search-helpers)\n32: - [D: Useful Constants and Defaults](#appendix-d-useful-constants-and-defaults)\n33: - [E: Unicode and UTF-8 Considerations for Code Editors](#appendix-e-unicode-and-utf-8-considerations-for-code-editors)\n34: - [F: Right-to-Left and Bidirectional Text Theory](#appendix-f-right-to-left-and-bidirectional-text-theory)\n35: \n36: ---\n37: \n38: ## Chapter 1: Foundational Concepts: The Scrollable Region\n39: \n40: ### 1.1 Core Entities\n41: \n42: At the most fundamental level, a scrollable region consists of these conceptual components:\n43: \n44: #### Content (Document / Scrollable Content)\n45: \n46: The full extent of what _could_ be displayed. Has its own coordinate system, often called **content coordinates** or\n47: **document coordinates**. For a text editor, this might be 50,000 pixels tall even though only 800 pixels are visible at\n48: any moment.\n49: \n50: #### Viewport (View / Visible Region)\n51: \n52: The rectangular \"window\" through which you see a portion of the content. Has a fixed size (width × height) and its own\n53: coordinate system where (0,0) is typically the top-left corner of what's currently visible.\n54: \n55: #### Scroll Offset (Scroll Position)\n56: \n57: The translation between content coordinates and viewport coordinates. Typically expressed as `(scrollX, scrollY)` — how\n58: far into the content the viewport's top-left corner is positioned.\n59: \n60: #### Scrollable Extent (Scroll Range / Scroll Bounds)\n61: \n62: The maximum distance the content can scroll. Always clamped to non-negative values:\n63: \n64: ```\n65: scrollableHeight = max(0, contentHeight - viewportHeight)\n66: scrollableWidth  = max(0, contentWidth - viewportWidth)\n67: ```\n68: \n69: When `contentHeight <= viewportHeight`, there is no vertical scroll (scrollableHeight = 0).\n70: \n71: ### 1.2 The Scrollbar Anatomy\n72: \n73: The **scrollbar** is the entire control assembly, typically containing:\n74: \n75: | Part           | Alternative Names                | Purpose                                          |\n76: | -------------- | -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ |\n77: | **Track**      | Gutter, Trough, Lane             | The full channel the thumb travels along         |\n78: | **Thumb**      | Handle, Knob, Scrubber, Elevator | The draggable indicator of current position      |\n79: | **Buttons**    | Arrows, Steppers                 | Optional increment/decrement buttons at ends     |\n80: | **Page Zones** | Gutter regions                   | Clickable areas above/below thumb for page jumps |\n81: \n82: ### 1.3 Key Mathematical Relationships\n83: \n84: #### Thumb Size (Proportional Scrollbars)\n85: \n86: ```\n87: thumbLength / trackLength = viewportSize / contentSize\n88: ```\n89: \n90: The thumb visually represents what fraction of content is visible.\n91: \n92: **Edge case:** When `contentSize <= viewportSize`, either hide the scrollbar entirely or set `thumbLength = trackLength`\n93: (thumb fills the track, indicating no scrolling possible).\n94: \n95: #### Scroll Position ↔ Thumb Position\n96: \n97: ```\n98: // Only valid when scrollableExtent > 0 and trackLength > thumbLength\n99: scrollOffset / scrollableExtent = thumbPosition / (trackLength - thumbLength)\n100: ```\n[... omitted lines 101 to ∞ ...]",

It stores the **column index** (not pixels) that\n263: the cursor should return to when possible.\n264: \n265: **Example:** You're at column 50, move down to a line with only 30 characters (cursor snaps to column 30), then move\n266: down again to a line with 100 characters. The cursor should return to column 50 because that's the `preferredColumn`.\n267: \n268: This is especially valuable when navigating through code with varying indentation levels or through blocks of code\n269: with different line lengths.\n270: \n271: ```\n272: function moveCursorDown(cursor, document) {\n273:     const nextLine = cursor.line + 1\n274:     if (nextLine >= document.lineCount) return cursor\n275: \n276:     const nextLineLength = document.lineLengths[nextLine]\n277:     const targetColumn = cursor.preferredColumn ?? cursor.column\n278: \n279:     return {\n280:         line: nextLine,\n281:         column: min(targetColumn, nextLineLength),\n282:         affinity: cursor.affinity,\n283:         preferredColumn: targetColumn  // preserve the original target\n284:     }\n285: }\n286: \n287: function moveCursorHorizontally(cursor, delta, document) {\n288:     // Horizontal movement resets preferredColumn\n289:     const newColumn = cursor.column + delta\n290:     return {\n291:         line: cursor.line,\n292:         column: clamp(newColumn, 0, document.lineLengths[cursor.line]),\n293:         affinity: cursor.affinity,\n294:         preferredColumn: null  // reset on horizontal movement\n295:     }\n296: }\n297: ```\n298: \n299: #### Selection\n300: \n301: A selection is defined by two positions: where it started and where the cursor currently is:\n302: \n303: ```\n304: Selection {\n305:     start: Position     // the lesser position (earlier in document)\n306:     end: Position       // the greater position (later in document)\n307:     direction: 'forward' | 'backward'  // indicates which end has the cursor\n308: }\n309: ```\n310: \n311: - When `direction == 'forward'`: cursor is at `end`, anchor is at `start`\n312: - When `direction == 'backward'`: cursor is at `start`, anchor is at `end`\n313: \n314: Helper functions for working with selections:\n315: \n316: ```\n317: function selectionHead(selection) {\n318:     return selection.direction == 'forward' ? selection.end : selection.start\n319: }\n320: \n321: function selectionAnchor(selection) {\n322:     return selection.direction == 'forward' ? selection.start : selection.end\n323: }\n324: \n325: function normalizeSelection(anchor, head) {\n326:     if (positionLessThan(anchor, head)) {\n327:         return { start: anchor, end: head, direction: 'forward' }\n328:     } else {\n329:         return { start: head, end: anchor, direction: 'backward' }\n330:     }\n331: }\n332: ```\n333: \n334: #### Editor Group / Pane Container\n335: \n336: A container that holds a tab bar and one editor pane. A horizontal split contains children arranged\n380: left-to-right; a vertical split arranges children top-to-bottom.\n381: \n382: ### 2.4 Multi-View Document Synchronization\n383: \n384: When the same document is open in multiple views (split or tabs):\n385: \n386: ```\n387: Document changes    ──────►  All views notified\n388:                                     │\n389:                     ┌───────────────┼───────────────┐\n390:                     ▼               ▼               ▼\n391:                  View A          View B          View C\n392: \n393: Each view independently decides:\n394: - How to adjust scroll (if edit was in another view)\n395: - Whether to reveal cursor (if cursor moved)\n396: - How to re-render affected lines\n397: ```\n398: \n399: Key considerations:\n400: \n401: - Edits in View A that affect lines visible in View B must trigger re-render in B\n402: - If View A inserts lines above View B's viewport, should View B scroll to maintain visual stability? (This is the\n403:   **scroll anchoring** problem)\n404: - Cursor positions are per-view, but document content is shared\n405: \n406: ---\n407: \n408: ## Chapter 3: The Gutter System\n409: \n410: The gutter is the vertical strip to the left of the text area, containing line numbers and various indicators.\n411: \n412: ### 3.1 Gutter Anatomy\n413: \n414: The gutter is a composite of multiple **gutter lanes** (or **gutter columns**):\n415: \n416: ```\n417: ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n418: │ Gutter                                  │ Text Area          │\n419: │ ┌────────┬─────┬──────┬───────────────┐ │                    │\n420: │ │Fold    │Break│ Line │ Git           │ │                    │\n421: │ │Markers │point│ Nums │ Annotations   │ │                    │\n422: │ ├────────┼─────┼──────┼───────────────┤ │                    │\n423: │ │  ▶     │  ●  │  42  │ ┃ (modified)  │ │ function foo() {   │\n424: │ │  ▼     │     │  43  │ ┃             │ │   // expanded...   │\n425: │ │        │     │  44  │               │ │   return bar; Now I have a good picture. Let me summarize:

### Current State Analysis

#### Status Bar - Current Implementation
The status bar is **very basic** - just a single formatted string with pipe separators:
```rust
// main.rs:541-543
format!(" {}{}  |  Ln {}, Col {}  |  {} lines  |  {}",
    file_name, modified, line, col, total_lines, model.ui.status_message)
```

**Issues:**
1. No structured segments - just string concatenation with `|`
2. `status_message` duplicates info (e.g., "Loaded: file.txt" when filename already shown)
3. No left/center/right alignment support
4. No theming per-segment

#### Multi-View/Split Panes - **NOT IMPLEMENTED**
According to EDITOR_UI_REFERENCE.md, you need:
- `EditorGroup` with tabs and panes
- `SplitContainer` with `horizontal`/`vertical` direction
- `LayoutNode` tree structure
- Multiple `EditorState` instances sharing one `Document`

**Currently:**
- Single `AppModel` with one `Document` + one `EditorState`
- No `EditorGroup`, `Tab`, `SplitContainer`, or layout tree
- No way to have two views of the same document

#### What Needs Implementation

| Feature | Status | Effort |
|---------|--------|--------|
| **Status Bar Segments** | ❌ Not done | Small |
| **EditorGroup / Tabs** | ❌ Not done | Medium |
| **SplitContainer** | ❌ Not done | Medium-Large |
| **Multi-view document sync** | ❌ Not done | Medium |
| **Layout tree rendering** | ❌ Not done | Large |

Would you like me to create a design document for the status bar segments and/or the split view architecture?

  "content": "# Status Bar Design\n\nA structured, segment-based status bar system replacing the current pipe-delimited string approach.\n\n---\n\n## Overview\n\n### Current Problems\n\n1. **String concatenation** - Status bar is built via `format!()` with `|` separators\n2. **Redundant info** - `status_message` often duplicates filename/state shown elsewhere\n3. **No alignment control** - Everything left-aligned, no right-side segments\n4. **No per-segment theming** - Single foreground color for all text\n5. **No interactivity** - Segments can't be clicked or hovered\n\n### Goals\n\n- **Structured segments** with defined positions (left, center, right)\n- **Typed segment content** (text, icon, clickable)\n- **Per-segment theming** with hover states\n- **Clean separation** of concerns (file info, cursor info, status messages, mode indicators)\n- **Extensible** for future features (encoding, line ending, language mode)\n\n---\n\n## Architecture\n\n### Data Structures\n\n```rust\n/// A segment in the status bar\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct StatusSegment {\n    /// Unique identifier for updates\n    pub id: SegmentId,\n    /// Content to display\n    pub content: SegmentContent,\n    /// Priority for overflow (higher = keep visible longer)\n    pub priority: u8,\n    /// Minimum width in characters (0 = flexible)\n    pub min_width: usize,\n    /// Click action (if interactive)\n    pub on_click: Option<SegmentAction>,\n}\n\n/// Segment identifier for targeted updates\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\npub enum SegmentId {\n    FileName,\n    ModifiedIndicator,\n    CursorPosition,\n    LineCount,\n    Selection,       // \"42 chars\" or \"5 lines\" when text selected\n    Encoding,        // UTF-8, UTF-16, etc.\n    LineEnding,      // LF, CRLF\n    Language,        // Rust, Python, etc.\n    GitBranch,       // Current branch\n    StatusMessage,   // Transient messages (\"Saved\", \"Loading...\")\n    EditorMode,      // Normal, Insert, Visual (for vim-style)\n    Custom(u32),     // For plugins/extensions\n}\n\n/// What to display in a segment\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum SegmentContent {\n    Text(String),\n    Icon { name: String, fallback: String },  // Icon with text fallback\n    TextWithIcon { icon: String, text: String },\n    Empty,  // Hidden/placeholder\n}\n\n/// Action when segment is clicked\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum SegmentAction {\n    OpenFilePicker,\n    ToggleLineEnding,\n    ChangeEncoding,\n    ShowGitMenu,\n    Custom(String),  // Named action for extensibility\n}\n\n/// Status bar layout\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct StatusBar {\n    /// Segments aligned to the left\n    pub left: Vec<StatusSegment>,\n    /// Segments in the center (optional)\n    pub center: Vec<StatusSegment>,\n    /// Segments aligned to the right\n    pub right: Vec<StatusSegment>,\n    /// Separator between segments\n    pub separator: String,\n    /// Padding on each side\n    pub padding: usize,\n}\n```\n\n### Default Layout\n\n```\n┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ LEFT                            Ln 42, Col 15 │ 1,234 Ln │\n└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### Segment Definitions\n\n```rust\nimpl StatusBar {\n    pub fn default_layout() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            left: vec![\n                StatusSegment {\n                    id: SegmentId::FileName,\n                    content: SegmentContent::Text(\"Untitled\".into()),\n                    priority: 100,  // Always show\n                    min_width: 0,\n                    on_click: Some(SegmentAction::OpenFilePicker),\n                },\n                StatusSegment {\n                    id: SegmentId::ModifiedIndicator,\n                    content: SegmentContent::Empty,  // Shows \"[+]\" when modified\n                    priority: 90,\n                    min_width: 0,\n                    on_click: None,\n                },\n                StatusSegment {\n                    id: SegmentId::StatusMessage,\n                    content: SegmentContent::Empty,  // Transient messages\n                    priority: 50,\n                    min_width: 0,\n                    on_click: None,\n                },\n            ],\n            center: vec![],  // Empty by default\n            right: vec![\n                StatusSegment {\n                    id: SegmentId::Selection,\n                    content: SegmentContent::Empty,  // Shows when text selected\n                    priority: 40,\n                    min_width: 0,\n                    on_click: None,\n                },\n                StatusSegment {\n                    id: SegmentId::CursorPosition,\n                    content: SegmentContent::Text(\"Ln 1, Col 1\".into()),\n                    priority: 80,\n                    min_width: 12,\n                    on_click: None,\n                },\n                StatusSegment {\n                    id: SegmentId::LineCount,\n                    content: SegmentContent::Text(\"1 Ln\".into()),\n                    priority: 60,\n                    min_width: 6,\n                    on_click: None,\n                },\n                StatusSegment {\n                    id: SegmentId::Encoding,\n                    content: SegmentContent::Text(\"UTF-8\".into()),\n                    priority: 30,\n                    min_width: 0,\n                    on_click: Some(SegmentAction::ChangeEncoding),\n                },\n                StatusSegment {\n                    id: SegmentId::LineEnding,\n                    content: SegmentContent::Text(\"LF\".into()),\n                    priority: 20,\n                    min_width: 0,\n                    on_click: }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Integration with Existing Architecture\n\n### UiState Changes\n\n```rust\n// In model/ui.rs\npub struct UiState {\n    /// Status bar with structured segments\n    pub status_bar: StatusBar,\n    \n    /// Transient message with auto-clear timer\n    pub transient_message: Option<TransientMessage>,\n    \n    // ... existing fields\n    pub cursor_visible: bool,\n MessageStyle,\n}\n\npub enum MessageStyle {\n    Info,\n    Success,\n    Warning,\n    Error,\n}\n```\n\n### New Messages\n\n```rust\n// In messages.rs\npub enum UiMsg {\n    // ... existing\n    SetStatus(String),\n    BlinkCursor,\n    \n    // New segment-based updates\n    UpdateSegment { id: SegmentId, content: SegmentContent },\n    SetTransientMessage { text: String, duration_ms: u64, style: MessageStyle },\n    ClearTransientMessage,\n    SegmentClicked(SegmentId),\n}\n```\n\n### Update Functions\n\n```rust\n// In update.rs\npub fn update_ui(model: &mut AppModel, msg: UiMsg) ->   model.ui.transient_message = None;\n            model.ui.status_bar.update_segment(\n                SegmentId::StatusMessage,\n                SegmentContent::Empty,\n            );\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n        \n        // ... other handlers\n    }\n}\n\n// Auto-update segments after document/cursor changes\npub fn sync_status_bar(model: &mut AppModel) {\n    let cursor = model.editor.cursor();\n    \n    // Cursor position\n    model.ui.status_bar.update_segment(\n        SegmentId::CursorPosition,\n        SegmentContent::Text(format!(\"Ln {}, Col {}\", cursor.line n.to_str())\n        .unwrap_or(\"Untitled\");\n    model.ui.status_bar.update_segment(\n        SegmentId::FileName,\n        SegmentContent::Text(file_name.into()),\n    );\n    \n    // Modified indicator\n    model.ui.status_bar.update_segment(\n        SegmentId::ModifiedIndicator,\n        if model.document.is_modified {\n            SegmentContent::Text(\"[+]\".into())\n        } else {\n     // Render left segments\n        let mut left_x = self.padding;\n        let mut left_segments = Vec::new();\n        \n        for (i, seg) in self.left.iter().enumerate() {\n            if let SegmentContent::Empty = seg.content {\n                continue;\n            }\n            if i > 0 {\n                left_x += separator_width;\n            }\n            let width = seg.content.char_width();\n            left_segments.push(RenderedSegment {\n                id: seg.id,\n                x: left_x,\n                width,\n                content: seg.content.clone(),\n            });\n            left_x += width;\n        }\n        \n        // Render right segments (from right edge, backwards)\n        let mut right_x = available_width - self.padding;\n        let mut right_segments = Vec::new();\n        \n        for (i, seg) in self.right.iter().rev().enumerate() {\n            if let SegmentContent::Empty = seg.content {\n                continue;\n            }\n            let width = seg.content.char_width();\n            if i > 0 {\n                right_x -= separator_width;\n            }\n            right_x -= width;\n            right_segments.push(RenderedSegment {\n                id: seg.id,\n                x: right_x,\n                width,\n                content: seg.content.clone(),\n            });\n        }\n        right_segments.reverse();\n        \n        StatusBarLayout {\n            left: left_segments,\n            center: vec![],  // TODO: center alignment\n            right: right_segments,\n            separator: self.separator.clone(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nstruct RenderedSegment {\n    id: SegmentId,\n    x: usize,  // Character position\n    width: usize,\n    content: SegmentContent,\n}\n\nstruct StatusBarLayout {\n    left: Vec<RenderedSegment>,\n    center: Vec<RenderedSegment>,\n    right: Vec<RenderedSegment>,\n    separator: String,\n}\n```\n\n### Theme Integration\n\n```rust\n// In theme.rs - extend StatusBarTheme\npub struct StatusBarTheme {\n    pub background: Color,\n    pub foreground: Color,\n    pub separator_color: Color,\n    \n    // Per-segment type colors\n    pub file_name: SegmentStyle,\n    pub modified_indicator: SegmentStyle,\n    pub cursor_position: SegmentStyle,\n    pub transient_message: TransientMessageStyles,\n}\n\npub struct SegmentStyle {\n    pub foreground: Color,\n    pub background: Option<Color>,  // For highlighted segments\n    pub foreground_hover: Option<Color>,\n}\n\npub struct TransientMessageStyles {\n    pub info: Color,\n    pub success: Color,\n    pub warning: Color,\n    pub error: Color,\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Plan\n\n### Phase 1: Core Data ├──────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────────┴────────────┤ │\n│  └─ LEFT ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── RIGHT ─────┘ │\n└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\nWith selection active:\n```\n│  main.rs [+]                        (42 chars)  Ln 42, Col 15  │  1,234 Ln   "content": "# Split View & Multi-Pane Architecture\n\nA hierarchical layout system for multiple editor panes, tabs, and split views.\n\n---\n\n## Overview\n\n### Current Limitations\n\nThe editor currently has:\n- Single `Document` in `AppModel`\n- Single `EditorState` (cursor, viewport, selections)\n- No concept of tabs or panes\n- No way to view the same document in two places\n- No split view capability\n\n### Goals\n\n- **Multiple editor panes** arranged in splits (horizontal/vertical)\n- **Tabs within each pane** for switching between documents\n- **Shared documents** - same file open in multiple views with synchronized edits\n- **Independent view state** - each pane has its own cursor, scroll position, selections\n- **Flexible layout tree** - arbitrary nesting of splits\n- **Focus management** - clear indication of active pane\n\n---\n\n## Architecture\n\n### Core Entities\n\nBased on EDITOR_UI_REFERENCE.md Chapter 2:\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ Window                                                                      │\n│ ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │\n│ │ EditorArea                                                              │ │\n│ │ ┌─────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────┐ │ │\n│ │ │ EditorGroup (Left)              │ EditorGroup (Right)               │ │ │\n│ │ │ ┌─────────────────────────────┐ │ ┌───────────────────────────────┐ │ │ │\n│ │ │ │ TabBar                      │ │ │ TabBar                        │ │ │ │\n│ │ │ │ [main.rs*][lib.rs]          │ │ │ [main.rs]                     │ │ │ │\n│ │ │ └─────────────────────────────┘ │ └───────────────────────────────┘ │ │ │\n│ │ │ ┌─────────────────────────────┐ │ ┌───────────────────────────────┐ │ │ │\n│ │ │ │ EditorPane                  │ │ │ EditorPane                    │ │ │ │\n│ │ │ │ (view of main.rs)           │ │ │ (another view of main.rs)     │ │ │ │\n│ │ │ │                             │ │ │                               │ │ │ │\n│ │ │ │ Independent scroll/cursor   │ │ │ Independent scroll/cursor     │ │ │ │\n│ │ │ └─────────────────────────────┘ │ └───────────────────────────────┘ │ │ │\n│ │ └─────────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────────┘ │ │\n│ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │\n│ ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │\n│ │ StatusBar (global or per-pane)                                          │ │\n│ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### Data Structures\n\n```rust\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Identifiers\n// ============================================================================\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\npub struct DocumentId(pub u64);\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\npub struct EditorId(pub u64);\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\npub struct GroupId(pub u64);\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\npub struct TabId(pub u64);\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Document (shared content)\n// ============================================================================\n\n/// A document is the shared text content, independent of how it's displayed.\n/// Multiple EditorStates can reference the same Document.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct Document {\n    pub id: DocumentId,\n    pub buffer: Rope,\n    pub file_path: Option<PathBuf>,\n    pub is_modified: bool,\n    pub undo_stack: Vec<EditOperation>,\n    pub redo_stack: Vec<EditOperation>,\n    \n    // Metadata\n    pub language: Option<String>,  // \"rust\", \"python\", etc.\n    pub encoding: Encoding,\n    pub line_ending: LineEnding,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]\npub enum Encoding {\n    #[default]\n    Utf8,\n    Utf16Le,\n    Utf16Be,\n    Latin1,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]\npub enum LineEnding {\n    #[default]\n    Lf,\n    CrLf,\n    Cr,\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Editor State (per-view state)\n// ============================================================================\n\n/// View-specific state for editing a document.\n/// Each EditorState references a Document but has independent cursor/scroll.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct EditorState {\n    pub id: EditorId,\n    pub document_id: DocumentId,\n    \n    // View state (independent per editor)\n    pub cursors: Vec<Cursor>,\n    pub selections: Vec<Selection>,\n    pub viewport: Viewport,\n    pub scroll_padding: usize,\n    \n    // View-specific settings\n    pub soft_wrap: bool,\n    pub show_line_numbers: bool,\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tabs\n// ============================================================================\n\n/// A tab represents an open editor in a group\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct Tab {\n    pub id: TabId,\n    pub editor_id: EditorId,\n    pub is_pinned: bool,\n    pub is_preview: bool,  // Preview tabs get replaced on next file open\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Editor Group (pane with tabs)\n// ============================================================================\n\n/// An editor group contains a tab bar and displays one editor at a time\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct EditorGroup {\n    pub id: GroupId,\n    pub tabs: Vec<Tab>,\n    pub active_tab_index: usize,\n    \n    // Layout info (set by parent)\n    pub rect: Rect,\n}\n\nimpl EditorGroup {\n    pub fn active_tab(&self) -> Option<&Tab> {\n        self.tabs.get(self.active_tab_index)\n    }\n    \n    pub fn active_editor_id(&self) -> Option<EditorId> {\n        self.active_tab().map(|t| t.editor_id)\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Layout Tree\n// ============================================================================\n\n/// A node in the layout tree - either a group or a split container\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum LayoutNode {\n    Group(GroupId),\n    Split(SplitContainer),\n}\n\n/// A container that splits space between children\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct SplitContainer {\n    pub direction: SplitDirection,\n    pub children: Vec<LayoutNode>,\n    /// Proportional sizes (0.0 to 1.0, must sum to 1.0)\n    pub ratios: Vec<f32>,\n    /// Minimum size in pixels for each child\n    pub min_sizes: Vec<f32>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum SplitDirection {\n    Horizontal,  // Children arranged left-to-right\n    Vertical,    // Children arranged top-to-bottom\n}\n\n/// Rectangle for layout calculations\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]\npub struct Rect {\n    pub x: f32,\n    pub y: f32,\n    pub width: f32,\n    pub height: f32,\n}\n\nimpl Rect {\n    pub fn new(x: f32, y: f32, width: f32, height: f32) -> Self {\n        Self { x, y, width, height }\n    }\n    \n    pub fn contains(&self, px: f32, py: f32) -> bool {\n        px >= self.x && px < self.x + self.width &&\n        py >= self.y && py < self.y + self.height\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Editor Area (top-level container)\n// ============================================================================\n\n/// The editor area manages all groups, documents, and the layout tree\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct EditorArea {\n    /// All open documents (shared across editors)\n    pub documents: HashMap<DocumentId, Document>,\n    \n    /// All editor states (each references a document)\n    pub editors: HashMap<EditorId, EditorState>,\n    \n    /// All editor groups (each contains tabs)\n    pub groups: HashMap<GroupId, EditorGroup>,\n    \n    /// The layout tree root\n    pub layout: LayoutNode,\n    \n    /// Currently focused group\n    pub focused_group_id: GroupId,\n    \n    /// ID generators\n    next_document_id: u64,\n    next_editor_id: u64,\n    next_group_id: u64,\n    next_tab_id: u64,\n}\n```\n\n### AppModel Changes\n\n```rust\n/// The complete application model\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct AppModel {\n    /// Editor area with all documents, editors, groups, and layout\n    pub editor_area: EditorArea,\n    \n    /// UI state (status bar, cursor blink, etc.)\n    pub ui: UiState,\n    \n    /// Theme\n    pub theme: Theme,\n    \n    /// Window dimensions\n    pub window_size: (u32, u32),\n    \n    /// Font metrics\n    pub line_height: usize,\n    pub char_width: f32,\n}\n\nimpl AppModel {\n    /// Get the currently focused document\n    pub fn focused_document(&self) -> Option<&Document> {\n        let group = self.editor_area.groups.get(&self.editor_area.focused_group_id)?;\n        let editor_id = group.active_editor_id()?;\n        let editor = self.editor_area.editors.get(&editor_id)?;\n        self.editor_area.documents.get(&editor.document_id)\n    }\n    \n    /// Get the currently focused editor state\n    pub fn focused_editor(&self) -> Option<&EditorState> {\n        let group = self.editor_area.groups.get(&self.editor_area.focused_group_id)?;\n        let editor_id = group.active_editor_id()?;\n        self.editor_area.editors.get(&editor_id)\n    }\n    \n    /// Get mutable focused editor\n    pub fn focused_editor_mut(&mut self) -> Option<&mut EditorState> {\n        let group = self.editor_area.groups.get(&self.editor_area.focused_group_id)?;\n        let editor_id = group.active_editor_id()?;\n        self.editor_area.editors.get_mut(&editor_id)\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Layout Algorithm\n\n### Computing Rectangles\n\n```rust\nimpl EditorArea {\n    /// Compute layout rectangles for all groups\n    pub fn compute_layout(&mut self, available_rect: Rect) {\n        self.compute_layout_node(&self.layout.clone(), available_rect);\n    }\n    \n    fn compute_layout_node(&mut self, node: &LayoutNode, rect: Rect) {\n        match node {\n            LayoutNode::Group(group_id) => {\n                if let Some(group) = self.groups.get_mut(group_id) {\n                    group.rect = rect;\n                }\n            }\n            LayoutNode::Split(split) => {\n                self.compute_split_layout(split, rect);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    fn compute_split_layout(&mut self, split: &SplitContainer, rect: Rect) {\n        let total_size = match split.direction {\n            SplitDirection::Horizontal => rect.width,\n            SplitDirection::Vertical => rect.height,\n        };\n        \n        // Splitter bar width\n        const SPLITTER_SIZE: f32 = 4.0;\n        let total_splitters = (split.children.len().saturating_sub(1)) as f32 * SPLITTER_SIZE;\n        let available = total_size - total_splitters;\n        \n        let mut offset = 0.0;\n        \n        for (i, (child, ratio)) in split.children.iter().zip(&split.ratios).enumerate() {\n            let child_size = available * ratio;\n            \n            let child_rect = match split.direction {\n                SplitDirection::Horizontal => Rect {\n                    x: rect.x + offset,\n                    y: rect.y,\n                    width: child_size,\n                    height: rect.height,\n                },\n                SplitDirection::Vertical => Rect {\n                    x: rect.x,\n                    y: rect.y + offset,\n                    width: rect.width,\n                    height: child_size,\n                },\n            };\n            \n            self.compute_layout_node(child, child_rect);\n            \n            offset += child_size;\n            if i < split.children.len() - 1 {\n                offset += SPLITTER_SIZE;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Hit Testing\n\n```rust\nimpl EditorArea {\n    /// Find which group contains a point\n    pub fn group_at_point(&self, x: f32, y: f32) -> Option<GroupId> {\n        self.group_at_point_in_node(&self.layout, x, y)\n    }\n    \n    fn group_at_point_in_node(&self, node: &LayoutNode, x: f32, y: f32) -> Option<GroupId> {\n        match node {\n            LayoutNode::Group(group_id) => {\n                let group = self.groups.get(group_id)?;\n                if group.rect.contains(x, y) {\n                    Some(*group_id)\n                } else {\n                    None\n                }\n            }\n            LayoutNode::Split(split) => {\n                for child in &split.children {\n                    if let Some(id) = self.group_at_point_in_node(child, x, y) {\n                        return Some(id);\n                    }\n                }\n                None\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Messages\n\n```rust\n/// Editor group / layout messages\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum LayoutMsg {\n    // Focus\n    FocusGroup(GroupId),\n    FocusNextGroup,\n    FocusPreviousGroup,\n    \n    // Tabs\n    OpenFile { path: PathBuf, group_id: Option<GroupId> },\n    CloseTab { group_id: GroupId, tab_id: TabId },\n    CloseActiveTab,\n    NextTab,\n    PreviousTab,\n    ActivateTab { group_id: GroupId, tab_index: usize },\n    MoveTabToGroup { tab_id: TabId, target_group_id: GroupId },\n    \n    // Splits\n    SplitHorizontal,  // Split focused group horizontally\n    SplitVertical,    // Split focused group vertically\n    CloseGroup(GroupId),\n    ResizeSplit { direction: SplitDirection, delta: f32 },\n    \n    // Layout\n    ResetLayout,  // Back to single pane\n}\n\n// Updated top-level Msg\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum Msg {\n    Editor(EditorMsg),\n    Document(DocumentMsg),\n    Ui(UiMsg),\n    App(AppMsg),\n    Layout(LayoutMsg),  // NEW\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Document Synchronization\n\nWhen the same document is open in multiple editors:\n\n```rust\nimpl EditorArea {\n    /// Apply an edit to a document and notify all editors viewing it\n    pub fn apply_edit(&mut self, document_id: DocumentId, edit: EditOperation) \n        -> Vec<EditorId> \n    {\n        // Apply to document\n        if let Some(doc) = self.documents.get_mut(&document_id) {\n            match &edit {\n                EditOperation::Insert { position, text, .. } => {\n                    doc.buffer.insert(*position, text);\n                }\n                EditOperation::Delete { position, text, .. } => {\n                    doc.buffer.remove(*position..*position + text.chars().count());\n                }\n            }\n            doc.push_edit(edit.clone());\n        }\n        \n        // Find all editors viewing this document\n        let affected_editors: Vec<EditorId> = self.editors\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|(_, e)| e.document_id == document_id)\n            .map(|(id, _)| *id)\n            .collect();\n        \n        // Each affected editor may need cursor/scroll adjustment\n        for editor_id in &affected_editors {\n            if let Some(editor) = self.editors.get_mut(editor_id) {\n                // Adjust cursor positions based on edit\n                self.adjust_cursors_for_edit(editor, &edit);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        affected_editors\n    }\n    \n    fn adjust_cursors_for_edit(&self, editor: &mut EditorState, edit: &EditOperation) {\n        // TODO: Implement cursor adjustment logic\n        // - If edit is before cursor, shift cursor\n        // - If edit is at cursor, decide behavior (typically cursor moves with insert)\n        // - If edit is after cursor, no change needed\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Rendering\n\n### Render Pipeline\n\n```rust\nimpl Renderer {\n    pub fn render(&mut self, model: &AppModel, perf: &PerfStats) -> Result<()> {\n        // Clear background\n        let bg_color = model.theme.editor.background.to_argb_u32();\n        self.buffer.fill(bg_color);\n        \n        // Compute layout\n        let editor_rect = Rect::new(\n            0.0, \n            0.0, \n            model.window_size.0 as f32, \n            model.window_size.1 as f32 - STATUS_BAR_HEIGHT,\n        );\n        \n        // Render each group\n        self.render_layout_node(&model.editor_area.layout, model)?;\n        \n        // Render splitter bars\n        self.render_splitters(&model.editor_area.layout, model)?;\n        \n        // Render status bar\n        self.render_status_bar(model)?;\n        \n        Ok(())\n    }\n    \n    fn render_layout_node(&mut self, node: &LayoutNode, model: &AppModel) -> Result<()> {\n        match node {\n            LayoutNode::Group(group_id) => {\n                if let Some(group) = model.editor_area.groups.get(group_id) {\n                    self.render_editor_group(group, model)?;\n                }\n            }\n            LayoutNode::Split(split) => {\n                for child in &split.children {\n                    self.render_layout_node(child, model)?;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n    \n    fn render_editor_group(&mut self, group: &EditorGroup, model: &AppModel) -> Result<()> {\n        let rect = group.rect;\n        let is_focused = group.id == model.editor_area.focused_group_id;\n        \n        // Tab bar height\n        const TAB_BAR_HEIGHT: f32 = 28.0;\n        \n        // Render tab bar\n        let tab_rect = Rect::new(rect.x, rect.y, rect.width, TAB_BAR_HEIGHT);\n        self.render_tab_bar(group, &tab_rect, model)?;\n        \n        // Render editor content\n        if let Some(editor_id) = group.active_editor_id() {\n            if let Some(editor) = model.editor_area.editors.get(&editor_id) {\n                if let Some(document) = model.editor_area.documents.get(&editor.document_id) {\n                    let content_rect = Rect::new(\n                        rect.x,\n                        rect.y + TAB_BAR_HEIGHT,\n                        rect.width,\n                        rect.height - TAB_BAR_HEIGHT,\n                    );\n                    self.render_editor_content(editor, document, &content_rect, is_focused, model)?;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // Focus indicator border\n        if is_focused {\n            self.render_focus_border(&rect, model)?;\n        }\n        \n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Tab Bar Rendering\n\n```rust\nfn render_tab_bar(&mut self, group: &EditorGroup, rect: &Rect, model: &AppModel) -> Result<()> {\n    // Background\n    let bg_color = model.theme.tab_bar.background.to_argb_u32();\n    self.fill_rect(rect, bg_color);\n    \n    let mut x = rect.x + 4.0;\n    \n    for (i, tab) in group.tabs.iter().enumerate() {\n        let is_active = i == group.active_tab_index;\n        let editor = model.editor_area.editors.get(&tab.editor_id);\n        let document = editor.and_then(|e| model.editor_area.documents.get(&e.document_id));\n        \n        let label = document\n            .and_then(|d| d.file_path.as_ref())\n            .and_then(|p| p.file_name())\n            .and_then(|n| n.to_str())\n            .unwrap_or(\"Untitled\");\n        \n        let modified = document.map(|d| d.is_modified).unwrap_or(false);\n        let display = if modified {\n            format!(\"{} •\", label)\n        } else {\n            label.to_string()\n        };\n        \n        // Tab background\n        let tab_bg = if is_active {\n            model.theme.tab_bar.active_background.to_argb_u32()\n        } else {\n            model.theme.tab_bar.inactive_background.to_argb_u32()\n        };\n        \n        let tab_width = (display.len() as f32 * self.char_width) + 16.0;\n        let tab_rect = Rect::new(x, rect.y, tab_width, rect.height);\n        self.fill_rect(&tab_rect, tab_bg);\n        \n        // Tab text\n        let text_color = if is_active {\n            model.theme.tab_bar.active_foreground.to_argb_u32()\n        } else {\n            model.theme.tab_bar.inactive_foreground.to_argb_u32()\n        };\n        \n        self.draw_text(x + 8.0, rect.y + 6.0, &display, text_color)?;\n        \n        x += tab_width + 2.0;\n    }\n    \n    Ok(())\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Theme Extensions\n\n```rust\n// In theme.rs\npub struct Theme {\n    pub name:
