Color,\n    pub active: Color,  // While dragging\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Plan\n\n### Phase 1: Core Data Structures\n- [ ] Add ID types (`DocumentId`, `EditorId`, `GroupId`, `TabId`)\n- [ ] Update `Document` to include `id: DocumentId`\n- [ ] Update `EditorState` to include `id: EditorId` and `document_id: DocumentId`\n- [ ] Add `Tab`, `EditorGroup`, `LayoutNode`, `SplitContainer`\n- [ ] Add `EditorArea` with HashMaps and layout tree\n\n### Phase 2: Layout System\n- [ ] Add `Rect` type\n- [ ] Implement `compute_layout()` for the layout tree\n- [ ] Implement `group_at_point()` for hit testing\n- [ ] Add splitter drag handling\n\n### Phase 3: Update AppModel\n- [ ] Replace single `Document`/`EditorState` with `EditorArea`\n- [ ] Add convenience methods (`focused_document()`, `focused_editor()`)\n- [ ] Update all `update_*` functions to work with focused editor\n\n### Phase 4: Messages\n- [ ] Add `LayoutMsg` enum\n- [ ] Add `update_layout()` function\n- [ ] Implement split/close/focus operations\n\n### Phase 5: Rendering\n- [ ] Update `Renderer` to iterate over groups\n- [ ] Implement `render_editor_group()`\n- [ ] Implement `render_tab_bar()`\n- [ ] Implement splitter bar rendering\n- [ ] Add focus indicator\n\n### Phase 6: Document Synchronization\n- [ ] Implement `apply_edit()` with multi-editor notification\n- [ ] Implement cursor adjustment for edits in other views\n- [ ] Handle undo/redo across shared documents\n\n### Phase 7: Keyboard Shortcuts\n- [ ] `Cmd+\\` - Split horizontal\n- [ ] `Cmd+Shift+\\` - Split vertical\n- [ ] `Cmd+W` - Close tab\n- [ ] `Cmd+Shift+W` - Close group\n- [ ] `Cmd+1/2/3` - Focus group by index\n- [ ] `Cmd+Tab` - Next tab\n- [ ] `Ctrl+Tab` - Next group\n\n---\n\n## Migration Path\n\nTo avoid breaking the existing single-pane functionality:\n\n1. **Create `EditorArea` with single group** - Wrap existing Document/EditorState\n2. **Keep `AppModel` convenience methods** - `focused_document()` returns what `model.document` used to\n3. **Gradually move rendering** - First render single group, then add splits\n4. **Add split commands only when rendering works**\n\n```rust\n// Initial migration - single group\nimpl EditorArea {\n    pub fn single_document(document: Document, editor: EditorState) -> Self {\n        let doc_id = DocumentId(1);\n        let editor_id = EditorId(1);\n        let group_id = GroupId(1);\n        let tab_id = TabId(1);\n        \n        let mut documents = HashMap::new();\n        let mut editors = HashMap::new();\n        let mut groups = HashMap::new();\n        \n        documents.insert(doc_id, Document { id: doc_id, ..document });\n        editors.insert(editor_id, EditorState { \n            id: editor_id, \n            document_id: doc_id, \n            ..editor \n        });\n        \n        let tab = Tab {\n            id: tab_id,\n            editor_id,\n            is_pinned: false,\n            is_preview: false,\n        };\n        \n        groups.insert(group_id, EditorGroup {\n            id: group_id,\n            tabs: vec![tab],\n            active_tab_index: 0,\n            rect: Rect::default(),\n        });\n        \n        Self {\n            documents,\n            editors,\n            groups,\n            layout: LayoutNode::Group(group_id),\n            focused_group_id: group_id,\n            next_document_id: 2,\n            next_editor_id: 2,\n            next_group_id: 2,\n            next_tab_id: 2,\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Example Scenarios\n\n### Split Current Editor Horizontally\n\n```\nBefore:                          After:\n┌────────────────────────┐       ┌───────────┬────────────┐\n│ [main.rs]              │       │ [main.rs] │ [main.rs]  │\n│                        │  ──►  │           │            │\n│ fn main() {            │       │ fn main() │ fn main()  │\n│     ...                │       │     ...   │     ...    │\n└────────────────────────┘       └───────────┴────────────┘\n\n- Creates new EditorGroup with same document\n- New EditorState with independent cursor/scroll\n- Layout changes from Group(1) to Split { children: [Group(1), Group(2)] }\n```\n\n### Open File in Split\n\n```\nUser: Cmd+\\ then opens lib.rs\n\n┌────────────────────────┐       ┌───────────┬────────────┐\n│ [main.rs]              │       │ [main.rs] │ [lib.rs]   │\n│                        │  ──►  │           │            │\n│ fn main() {            │       │ fn main() │ mod utils; │\n└────────────────────────┘       └───────────┴────────────┘\n```\n\n### Edit Synchronized Across Views\n\n```\n┌───────────┬────────────┐\n│ [main.rs] │ [main.rs]  │   Both views show same Document\n│           │            │   \n│ fn foo()▌ │ fn foo()   │   Left view: cursor at line 1\n│           │            │   Right view: scrolled to line 50\n│           │            │\n└───────────┴────────────┘\n\nUser types in left view:\n- Document.buffer updated\n- Left EditorState cursor advances\n- Right EditorState notified, may need cursor adjustment\n- Both views re-render\n```\n"
}
```

  "content": "# Selection & Multi-Cursor Design\n\nComprehensive design for text selection and multi-cursor editing with JetBrains-style keyboard shortcuts.\n\n---\n\n## Overview\n\n### Current State\n\nThe codebase already has foundational types in `model/editor.rs`:\n\n```rust\n// Already implemented\npub struct Position { pub line: usize, pub column: usize }\npub struct Selection { pub anchor: Position, pub head: Position }\npub struct Cursor { pub line: usize, pub column: usize, pub desired_column: Option<usize> }\npub struct EditorState {\n    pub cursors: Vec<Cursor>,\n    pub selections: Vec<Selection>,  // Parallel to cursors\n    // ...\n}\n```\n\n**What's missing:**\n- Selection movement (Shift+Arrow)\n- Selection rendering\n- Multi-cursor editing logic\n- Rectangle selection\n- Clipboard operations with selections\n- Word/line selection\n\n### Goals\n\n| Feature | Description |\n|---------|-------------|\n| **Text Selection** | Shift+movement extends selection from anchor |\n| **Multi-Cursor** | Multiple independent cursors editing simultaneously |\n| **Rectangle Select** | Column-mode selection via middle mouse drag |\n| **Clone Cursor** | Add cursor above/below via Option+Option+Arrow |\n| **Clipboard** | Cut/copy/paste respecting selections |\n| **Word/Line Select** | Double-click word, triple-click line, Cmd+D next match |\n\n---\n\n## Part 1: Selection Model\n\n### Selection Semantics\n\nA selection has two key positions:\n- **Anchor**: Where the selection started (fixed)\n- **Head**: Where the cursor currently is (moves with arrow keys)\n\n```\nForward selection (head after anchor):\n    anchor          head\n       │              │\n       ▼              ▼\n       Hello, World!\n       └──────────────┘\n         selected text\n\nBackward selection (head before anchor):\n       head          anchor\n       │              │\n       ▼              ▼\n       Hello, World!\n       └──────────────┘\n         selected text\n```\n\n**Direction preservation** is critical:\n- Shift+Right with forward selection: head moves right\n- Shift+Left with forward selection: head moves left (may cross anchor)\n- When head crosses anchor, selection reverses direction\n\n### Selection State\n\nEach cursor has a parallel selection:\n\n```rust\n// In EditorState\npub cursors: Vec<Cursor>,      // Cursor positions\npub selections: Vec<Selection>, // Parallel selections (cursors[i] pairs with selections[i])\n\n// Invariant: cursors.len() == selections.len()\n// Invariant: cursors[i].to_position() == selections[i].head\n```\n\n### Selection Operations\n\n```rust\nimpl Selection {\n    /// Extend selection by moving head to new position\n    pub fn extend_to(&mut self, new_head: Position) {\n        self.head = new_head;\n    }\n    \n    /// Collapse selection to cursor position (clear selection)\n    pub fn collapse_to_head(&mut self) {\n        self.anchor = self.head;\n    }\n    \n    /// Collapse to anchor (used when pressing left with selection)\n    pub fn collapse_to_start(&mut self) {\n        let start = self.start();\n        self.anchor = start;\n        self.head = start;\n    }\n    \n    /// Collapse to end (used when pressing right with selection)\n    pub fn collapse_to_end(&mut self) {\n        let end = self.end();\n        self.anchor = end;\n        self.head = end;\n    }\n    \n    /// Get selected text from document\n    pub fn get_text(&self, document: &Document) -> String {\n        if self.is_empty() {\n            return String::new();\n        }\n        let start = self.start();\n        let end = self.end();\n        let start_offset = document.cursor_to_offset(start.line, start.column);\n        let end_offset = document.cursor_to_offset(end.line, end.column);\n        document.buffer.slice(start_offset..end_offset).to_string()\n    }\n    \n    /// Check if position is within selection\n    pub fn contains(&self, pos: Position) -> bool {\n        pos >= self.start() && pos < self.end()\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Part 2: Keyboard Shortcuts\n\n### Selection Movement\n\n| Shortcut | Action | Message |\n|----------|--------|---------|\n| Shift+← | Extend selection left | `EditorMsg::MoveCursorWithSelection(Left)` |\n| Shift+→ | Extend selection right | `EditorMsg::MoveCursorWithSelection(Right)` |\n| Shift+↑ | Extend selection up | `EditorMsg::MoveCursorWithSelection(Up)` |\n| Shift+↓ | Extend selection down | `EditorMsg::MoveCursorWithSelection(Down)` |\n| Shift+Home | Extend to line start | `EditorMsg::MoveCursorLineStartWithSelection` |\n| Shift+End | Extend to line end | `EditorMsg::MoveCursorLineEndWithSelection` |\n| Shift+Cmd+← | Extend word left | `EditorMsg::MoveCursorWordWithSelection(Left)` |\n| Shift+Cmd+→ | Extend word right | `EditorMsg::MoveCursorWordWithSelection(Right)` |\n| Cmd+A | Select all | `EditorMsg::SelectAll` |\n| Escape | Clear selection | `EditorMsg::ClearSelection` |\n\n### Movement with Selection\n\nWhen moving **without** Shift and a selection exists:\n- **Left/Up**: Collapse to selection start, then move\n- **Right/Down**: Collapse to selection end, then move\n\n```rust\nfn move_cursor_left(model: &mut AppModel, extend_selection: bool) {\n    let selection = model.editor.selection_mut();\n    \n    if extend_selection {\n        // Move head left, anchor stays\n        let new_pos = compute_position_left(selection.head, &model.document);\n        selection.head = new_pos;\n        model.editor.cursor_mut().line = new_pos.line;\n        model.editor.cursor_mut().column = new_pos.column;\n    } else if !selection.is_empty() {\n        // Collapse to start\n        let start = selection.start();\n        selection.collapse_to_start();\n        model.editor.cursor_mut().line = start.line;\n        model.editor.cursor_mut().column = start.column;\n    } else {\n        // Normal movement\n        move_cursor_left_impl(model);\n        selection.collapse_to_head();\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Multi-Cursor Shortcuts\n\n| Shortcut | Action | Message |\n|----------|--------|---------|\n| Cmd+Click | Toggle cursor at position | `EditorMsg::ToggleCursorAtPosition { line, column }` |\n| Cmd+D | Select next occurrence of word/selection | `EditorMsg::SelectNextOccurrence` |\n| Cmd+Shift+L | Select all occurrences | `EditorMsg::SelectAllOccurrences` |\n| Option+Option+↑ | Add cursor above | `EditorMsg::AddCursorAbove` |\n| Option+Option+↓ | Add cursor below | `EditorMsg::AddCursorBelow` |\n| Escape | Collapse to single cursor | `EditorMsg::CollapseToSingleCursor` |\n\n### Word & Line Selection\n\n| Action | Trigger | Behavior |\n|--------|---------|----------|\n| Select word | Double-click | Select word under cursor |\n| Select line | Triple-click | Select entire line including newline |\n| Expand selection | Cmd+W (JetBrains) | Expand: word → quotes → brackets → block |\n\n---\n\n## Part 3: Messages\n\n```rust\n// In messages.rs\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum EditorMsg {\n    // Existing movement\n    MoveCursor(Direction),\n    MoveCursorLineStart,\n    MoveCursorLineEnd,\n    MoveCursorDocumentStart,\n    MoveCursorDocumentEnd,\n    MoveCursorWord(Direction),\n    PageUp,\n    PageDown,\n    SetCursorPosition { line: usize, column: usize },\n    Scroll(i32),\n    ScrollHorizontal(i32),\n    \n    // === NEW: Selection Movement ===\n    MoveCursorWithSelection(Direction),\n    MoveCursorLineStartWithSelection,\n    MoveCursorLineEndWithSelection,\n    MoveCursorDocumentStartWithSelection,\n    MoveCursorDocumentEndWithSelection,\n    MoveCursorWordWithSelection(Direction),\n    PageUpWithSelection,\n    PageDownWithSelection,\n    \n    // === NEW: Selection Commands ===\n    SelectAll,\n    SelectWord,                          // Select word at cursor\n    SelectLine,                          // Select entire line\n    ExtendSelectionToPosition { line: usize, column: usize },  // Shift+Click\n    ClearSelection,                      // Collapse all selections\n    \n    // === NEW: Multi-Cursor ===\n    ToggleCursorAtPosition { line: usize, column: usize },  // Cmd+Click\n    AddCursorAbove,                      // Option+Option+Up\n    AddCursorBelow,                      // Option+Option+Down\n    CollapseToSingleCursor,              // Escape with multiple cursors\n    RemoveCursor(usize),                 // Remove cursor by index\n    \n    // === NEW: Find & Select ===\n    SelectNextOccurrence,                // Cmd+D\n    SelectAllOccurrences,                // Cmd+Shift+L\n    \n    // === NEW: Rectangle Selection ===\n    StartRectangleSelection { line: usize, column: usize },\n    UpdateRectangleSelection { line: usize, column: usize },\n    FinishRectangleSelection,\n    CancelRectangleSelection,\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Part 4: Multi-Cursor Editing\n\n### Cursor Ordering\n\nCursors must be processed in **reverse document order** (highest offset first) to prevent edits from shifting subsequent cursor positions.\n\n```rust\n/// Get cursor indices sorted by document offset descending\nfn cursors_in_edit_order(editor: &EditorState, document: &Document) -> Vec<usize> {\n    let mut indices: Vec<usize> = (0..editor.cursors.len()).collect();\n    indices.sort_by(|&a, &b| {\n        let offset_a = document.cursor_to_offset(\n            editor.cursors[a].line, \n            editor.cursors[a].column\n        );\n        let offset_b = document.cursor_to_offset(\n            editor.cursors[b].line, \n            editor.cursors[b].column\n        );\n        offset_b.cmp(&offset_a)  // Descending\n    });\n    indices\n}\n```\n\n### Insert Character at All Cursors\n\n```rust\nfn insert_char_multi(model: &mut AppModel, ch: char) {\n    let indices = cursors_in_edit_order(&model.editor, &model.document);\n    \n    for &i in &indices {\n        let cursor = &model.editor.cursors[i];\n        let selection = &model.editor.selections[i];\n        \n        // If selection exists, delete it first\n        if !selection.is_empty() {\n            delete_selection(model, i);\n        }\n        \n        // Insert character\n        let pos = model.document.cursor_to_offset(cursor.line, cursor.column);\n        model.document.buffer.insert_char(pos, ch);\n        \n        // Move cursor right\n        model.editor.cursors[i].column += 1;\n        model.editor.selections[i] = Selection::new(\n            model.editor.cursors[i].to_position()\n        );\n    }\n    \n    // Record undo operation (combined for all cursors)\n    // ...\n    \n    deduplicate_cursors(model);\n}\n```\n\n### Cursor Deduplication\n\nAfter operations, cursors may overlap. Remove duplicates while preserving primary cursor (index 0).\n\n```rust\nfn deduplicate_cursors(model: &mut AppModel) {\n    let mut seen: HashSet<(usize, usize)> = HashSet::new();\n    let mut to_remove: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();\n    \n    // Always keep primary cursor\n    seen.insert((model.editor.cursors[0].line, model.editor.cursors[0].column));\n    \n    for i in 1..model.editor.cursors.len() {\n        let key = (model.editor.cursors[i].line, model.editor.cursors[i].column);\n        if seen.contains(&key) {\n            to_remove.push(i);\n        } else {\n            seen.insert(key);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Remove in reverse order to preserve indices\n    for i in to_remove.into_iter().rev() {\n        model.editor.cursors.remove(i);\n        model.editor.selections.remove(i);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Selection Merging\n\nWhen selections overlap, merge them:\n\n```rust\nfn merge_overlapping_selections(editor: &mut EditorState) {\n    if editor.selections.len() <= 1 {\n        return;\n    }\n    \n    // Sort by start position\n    let mut indexed: Vec<(usize, Selection)> = editor.selections\n        .iter()\n        .cloned()\n        .enumerate()\n        .collect();\n    indexed.sort_by_key(|(_, s)| s.start());\n    \n    let mut merged: Vec<(usize, Selection)> = Vec::new();\n    \n    for (i, sel) in indexed {\n        if let Some((_, last)) = merged.last_mut() {\n            if sel.start() <= last.end() {\n                // Overlapping - extend last selection\n                if sel.end() > last.end() {\n                    last.head = sel.end();  // Extend to cover both\n                }\n                // Remove the cursor for this merged selection\n                // (handled separately)\n                continue;\n            }\n        }\n        merged.push((i, sel));\n    }\n    \n    // Rebuild cursors and selections from merged set\n    // Keep only cursors whose selections weren't merged away\n    // ...\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Part 5: Rectangle Selection\n\n### State\n\n```rust\n// In EditorState\npub struct EditorState {\n    // ... existing fields\n    \n    /// Rectangle selection state (None when not in rectangle mode)\n    pub rectangle_selection: Option<RectangleSelectionState>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\npub struct RectangleSelectionState {\n    /// Starting corner of rectangle\n    pub anchor: Position,\n    /// Current corner (follows mouse)\n    pub current: Position,\n}\n```\n\n### Algorithm\n\n```rust\nfn update_rectangle_selection(model: &mut AppModel, current: Position) {\n    let Some(rect_state) = &model.editor.rectangle_selection else {\n        return;\n    };\n    \n    let anchor = rect_state.anchor;\n    \n    // Compute rectangle bounds\n    let start_line = anchor.line.min(current.line);\n    let end_line = anchor.line.max(current.line);\n    let start_col = anchor.column.min(current.column);\n    let end_col = anchor.column.max(current.column);\n    \n    // Clear existing cursors/selections\n    model.editor.cursors.clear();\n    model.editor.selections.clear();\n    \n    // Create cursor/selection on each line within bounds\n    for line in start_line..=end_line {\n        let line_len = model.document.line_length(line);\n        \n        // Clamp columns to line length\n        let clamped_start = start_col.min(line_len);\n        let clamped_end = end_col.min(line_len);\n        \n        // Cursor at the \"current\" side of rectangle\n        let cursor_col = if current.column > anchor.column {\n            clamped_end\n        } else {\n            clamped_start\n        };\n        \n        model.editor.cursors.push(Cursor::at(line, cursor_col));\n        \n        // Selection covers the rectangle width (or empty if zero-width)\n        if clamped_start != clamped_end {\n            model.editor.selections.push(Selection::from_anchor_head(\n                Position::new(line, clamped_start),\n                Position::new(line, clamped_end),\n            ));\n        } else {\n            // Zero-width: empty selection (just cursor)\n            model.editor.selections.push(Selection::new(\n                Position::new(line, cursor_col)\n            ));\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Update the rectangle state\n    model.editor.rectangle_selection = Some(RectangleSelectionState {\n        anchor,\n        current,\n    });\n}\n```\n\n### Visual Feedback\n\nDuring rectangle selection, render:\n1. Semi-transparent rectangle overlay showing bounds\n2. Cursors on each line (even if clamped)\n3. Selection backgrounds if width > 0\n\n```rust\nfn render_rectangle_selection_overlay(\n    buffer: &mut [u32],\n    rect_state: &RectangleSelectionState,\n    model: &AppModel,\n    // ... rendering params\n) {\n    let anchor = rect_state.anchor;\n    let current = rect_state.current;\n    \n    // Convert to screen coordinates\n    let x1 = column_to_pixel(anchor.column.min(current.column));\n    let x2 = column_to_pixel(anchor.column.max(current.column));\n    let y1 = line_to_pixel(anchor.line.min(current.line));\n    let y2 = line_to_pixel(anchor.line.max(current.line) + 1);  // +1 for bottom of last line\n    \n    // Draw semi-transparent rectangle border\n    let border_color = model.theme.editor.selection_border.to_argb_u32();\n    draw_rect_outline(buffer, x1, y1, x2, y2, border_color);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Part 6: Double-Tap Option Detection\n\n### State\n\n```rust\n// In App (event loop state, not EditorState)\nstruct DoubleTapDetector {\n    last_key_down: Option<(Key, Instant)>,\n    is_held: bool,\n}\n\nimpl DoubleTapDetector {\n    const DOUBLE_TAP_WINDOW: Duration = Duration::from_millis(300);\n    \n    fn on_key_down(&mut self, key: Key) -> bool {\n        let now = Instant::now();\n        \n        if let Some((last_key, last_time)) = &self.last_key_down {\n            if *last_key == key && now.duration_since(*last_time) < Self::DOUBLE_TAP_WINDOW {\n                // Double-tap detected!\n                self.is_held = true;\n                self.last_key_down = None;\n                return true;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        self.last_key_down = Some((key, now));\n        false\n    }\n    \n    fn on_key_up(&mut self, key: Key) {\n        if self.is_held {\n            self.is_held = false;\n        }\n        if let Some((last_key, _)) = &self.last_key_down {\n            if *last_key == key {\n                // Key released before double-tap\n                // Keep last_key_down for potential second tap\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    fn is_in_add_cursor_mode(&self) -> bool {\n        self.is_held\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Integration\n\n```rust\n// In handle_key\nif self.option_detector.is_in_add_cursor_mode() {\n    match key {\n        Key::Named(NamedKey::ArrowUp) => {\n            return Some(Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::AddCursorAbove));\n        }\n        Key::Named(NamedKey::ArrowDown) => {\n            return Some(Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::AddCursorBelow));\n        }\n        _ => {}\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Part 7: Clipboard Operations\n\n### Copy\n\n```rust\nfn copy_selections(model: &AppModel) -> String {\n    if model.editor.cursors.len() == 1 {\n        // Single cursor: copy selection or current line\n        let selection = model.editor.selection();\n        if selection.is_empty() {\n            // Copy entire line\n            model.document.get_line(model.editor.cursor().line)\n                .unwrap_or_default()\n        } else {\n            selection.get_text(&model.document)\n        }\n    } else {\n        // Multi-cursor: join all selections with newlines\n        model.editor.selections.iter()\n            .map(|s| s.get_text(&model.document))\n            .collect::<Vec<_>>()\n            .join(\"\\n\")\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Paste\n\n```rust\nfn paste_at_cursors(model: &mut AppModel, text: &str) {\n    let lines: Vec<&str> = text.lines().collect();\n    let indices = cursors_in_edit_order(&model.editor, &model.document);\n    \n    if lines.len() == model.editor.cursors.len() {\n        // Multi-line paste to multi-cursor: one line per cursor\n        for (i, &cursor_idx) in indices.iter().enumerate() {\n            let line_to_paste = lines.get(i).unwrap_or(&\"\");\n            insert_text_at_cursor(model, cursor_idx, line_to_paste);\n        }\n    } else {\n        // Paste same text at all cursors\n        for &cursor_idx in &indices {\n            insert_text_at_cursor(model, cursor_idx, text);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    deduplicate_cursors(model);\n}\n```\n\n### Cut\n\n```rust\nfn cut_selections(model: &mut AppModel) -> String {\n    let copied = copy_selections(model);\n    \n    // Delete all selections (in reverse order)\n    let indices = cursors_in_edit_order(&model.editor, &model.document);\n    for &i in &indices {\n        if !model.editor.selections[i].is_empty() {\n            delete_selection(model, i);\n        } else {\n            // No selection: cut entire line\n            delete_line(model, i);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    deduplicate_cursors(model);\n    copied\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Part 8: Theme Integration\n\n```rust\n// In theme.rs\n\npub struct EditorTheme {\n    pub background: Color,\n    pub foreground: Color,\n    pub current_line_background: Color,\n    pub cursor_color: Color,\n    \n    // NEW: Selection colors\n    pub selection_background: Color,\n    pub selection_background_inactive: Color,  // Unfocused window\n    pub selection_border: Option<Color>,       // Optional border\n    \n    // NEW: Multi-cursor indicators\n    pub secondary_cursor_color: Color,         // Non-primary cursors\n}\n```\n\n### Default Colors\n\n```rust\n// In Theme::default_dark()\nEditorTheme {\n    // ... existing\n    selection_background: Color::rgb(0x26, 0x4F, 0x78),        // #264F78 (VSCode blue)\n    selection_background_inactive: Color::rgb(0x1D, 0x3A, 0x5C), // Dimmer\n    selection_border: None,\n    secondary_cursor_color: Color::rgba(0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF, 0x80), // Semi-transparent\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Part 9: Rendering\n\n### Selection Rendering Order\n\n```\n1. Clear background\n2. Draw current line highlight(s) - one per cursor\n3. Draw selections (background color)\n4. Draw text\n5. Draw cursors (all of them)\n6. Draw rectangle selection overlay (if active)\n```\n\n### Selection Rendering\n\n```rust\nfn render_selections(\n    buffer: &mut [u32],\n    model: &AppModel,\n    viewport: &Viewport,\n    line_height: usize,\n    char_width: f32,\n    text_start_x: usize,\n) {\n    let selection_bg = model.theme.editor.selection_background.to_argb_u32();\n    \n    for selection in &model.editor.selections {\n        if selection.is_empty() {\n            continue;\n        }\n        \n        let start = selection.start();\n        let end = selection.end();\n        \n        // For each line in selection\n        for line in start.line..=end.line {\n            // Skip if line not in viewport\n            if line < viewport.top_line || line >= viewport.top_line + viewport.visible_lines {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            let screen_line = line - viewport.top_line;\n            let y = screen_line * line_height;\n            \n            // Calculate selection bounds on this line\n            let line_len = model.document.line_length(line);\n            \n            let sel_start_col = if line == start.line { start.column } else { 0 };\n            let sel_end_col = if line == end.line { end.column } else { line_len };\n            \n            // Adjust for horizontal scroll\n            let visible_start = sel_start_col.saturating_sub(viewport.left_column);\n            let visible_end = sel_end_col.saturating_sub(viewport.left_column);\n            \n            if visible_end <= visible_start {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            let x1 = text_start_x + (visible_start as f32 * char_width) as usize;\n            let x2 = text_start_x + (visible_end as f32 * char_width) as usize;\n            \n            // Fill selection rectangle\n            fill_rect(buffer, x1, y, x2 - x1, line_height, selection_bg);\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Multi-Cursor Rendering\n\n```rust\nfn render_cursors(\n    buffer: &mut [u32],\n    model: &AppModel,\n    viewport: &Viewport,\n    line_height: usize,\n    char_width: f32,\n    text_start_x: usize,\n) {\n    if !model.ui.cursor_visible {\n        return;  // Blink off\n    }\n    \n    for (i, cursor) in model.editor.cursors.iter().enumerate() {\n        // Skip if not in viewport\n        if cursor.line < viewport.top_line || \n           cursor.line >= viewport.top_line + viewport.visible_lines {\n            continue;\n        }\n        \n        let screen_line = cursor.line - viewport.top_line;\n        let screen_col = cursor.column.saturating_sub(viewport.left_column);\n        \n        let x = text_start_x + (screen_col as f32 * char_width) as usize;\n        let y = screen_line * line_height;\n        \n        // Primary cursor uses main color, secondary uses dimmer color\n        let cursor_color = if i == 0 {\n            model.theme.editor.cursor_color.to_argb_u32()\n        } else {\n            model.theme.editor.secondary_cursor_color.to_argb_u32()\n        };\n        \n        // Draw pipe cursor (2px wide)\n        fill_rect(buffer, x, y, 2, line_height, cursor_color);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Part 10: Undo/Redo with Multi-Cursor\n\n### Enhanced EditOperation\n\n```rust\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum EditOperation {\n    Insert {\n        position: usize,\n        text: String,\n        cursor_before: Cursor,\n        cursor_after: Cursor,\n    },\n    Delete {\n        position: usize,\n        text: String,\n        cursor_before: Cursor,\n        cursor_after: Cursor,\n    },\n    // NEW: Multi-cursor batch operation\n    Batch {\n        operations: Vec<EditOperation>,\n        cursors_before: Vec<Cursor>,\n        selections_before: Vec<Selection>,\n        cursors_after: Vec<Cursor>,\n        selections_after: Vec<Selection>,\n    },\n}\n```\n\n### Recording Batch Operations\n\n```rust\nfn record_multi_cursor_edit(\n    model: &mut AppModel,\n    operations: Vec<EditOperation>,\n    cursors_before: Vec<Cursor>,\n    selections_before: Vec<Selection>,\n) {\n    let cursors_after = model.editor.cursors.clone();\n    let selections_after = model.editor.selections.clone();\n    \n    model.document.push_edit(EditOperation::Batch {\n        operations,\n        cursors_before,\n        selections_before,\n        cursors_after,\n        selections_after,\n    });\n}\n```\n\n### Undo Batch\n\n```rust\nfn undo_batch(model: &mut AppModel, batch: &EditOperation::Batch) {\n    // Undo individual operations in reverse order\n    for op in batch.operations.iter().rev() {\n        match op {\n            EditOperation::Insert { position, text, .. } => {\n                model.document.buffer.remove(*position..*position + text.chars().count());\n            }\n            EditOperation::Delete { position, text, .. } => {\n                model.document.buffer.insert(*position, text);\n            }\n            _ => {}\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Restore cursors and selections\n    model.editor.cursors = batch.cursors_before.clone();\n    model.editor.selections = batch.selections_before.clone();\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Plan\n\n### Phase 1: Basic Selection (Foundation)\n- [ ] Add `selection_background` to theme\n- [ ] Implement `MoveCursorWithSelection(Direction)` messages\n- [ ] Update `handle_key()` for Shift+Arrow detection\n- [ ] Render selections before text\n- [ ] Handle Shift+Click for `ExtendSelectionToPosition`\n- [ ] Implement selection collapse on non-shift movement\n- [ ] Escape clears selection\n\n**Test:** Shift+Arrow creates visible selection, movement without shift collapses it.\n\n### Phase 2: Selection Editing\n- [ ] Delete selection on Backspace/Delete\n- [ ] Replace selection when typing\n- [ ] Update `InsertChar` to delete selection first\n- [ ] Update `InsertNewline` to delete selection first\n- [ ] Handle selection with word delete (Option+Backspace)\n\n**Test:** Type with selection replaces text.\n\n### Phase 3: Word & Line Selection\n- [ ] Implement `SelectWord` (double-click or Cmd+W)\n- [ ] Implement `SelectLine` (triple-click)\n- [ ] Implement `SelectAll` (Cmd+A)\n- [ ] Add word boundary detection for selection\n\n**Test:** Double-click selects word, triple-click selects line.\n\n### Phase 4: Multi-Cursor Basics\n- [ ] Implement `ToggleCursorAtPosition` for Cmd+Click\n- [ ] Render all cursors (with secondary color)\n- [ ] Highlight all cursor lines\n- [ ] Implement `CollapseToSingleCursor` on Escape\n- [ ] Add cursor deduplication\n\n**Test:** Cmd+Click adds/removes cursors, Escape collapses.\n\n### Phase 5: Multi-Cursor Editing\n- [ ] Implement reverse-order editing for all cursors\n- [ ] Handle offset adjustments for subsequent cursors\n- [ ] Implement selection merging\n- [ ] Update all edit operations (insert, delete, newline)\n- [ ] Add batch undo/redo support\n\n**Test:** Type with multiple cursors, all insert correctly.\n\n### Phase 6: Clipboard\n- [ ] Implement `copy_selections()` \n- [ ] Implement `paste_at_cursors()` with line matching\n- [ ] Implement `cut_selections()`\n- [ ] Handle empty selection copy (copy line)\n\n**Test:** Copy/paste works with multiple cursors.\n\n### Phase 7: Rectangle Selection\n- [ ] Add `RectangleSelectionState` to EditorState\n- [ ] Handle middle mouse down/drag/up\n- [ ] Implement `update_rectangle_selection()` algorithm\n- [ ] Render rectangle overlay during drag\n- [ ] Handle line clamping for short lines\n\n**Test:** Middle-drag creates cursors on each line.\n\n### Phase 8: Double-Tap Option\n- [ ] Add `DoubleTapDetector` to App\n- [ ] Track Option key press/release\n- [ ] Implement `AddCursorAbove` / `AddCursorBelow`\n- [ ] Exit add-cursor mode on other keys\n\n**Test:** Double-tap Option, hold, Up/Down adds cursors.\n\n### Phase 9: Find & Select\n- [ ] Implement `SelectNextOccurrence` (Cmd+D)\n- [ ] Implement `SelectAllOccurrences` (Cmd+Shift+L)\n- [ ] Add word-under-cursor detection\n- [ ] Handle case sensitivity option\n\n**Test:** Cmd+D selects next occurrence of word.\n\n---\n\n## Files to Modify\n\n| File | Changes |\n|------|---------|\n| `src/theme.rs` | Add selection colors to EditorTheme |\n| `src/messages.rs` | Add ~20 new EditorMsg variants |\n| `src/model/editor.rs` | Add RectangleSelectionState, selection helpers |\n| `src/model/document.rs` | Add EditOperation::Batch variant |\n| `src/update.rs` | Selection movement, multi-cursor editing logic |\n| `src/main.rs` | Selection/cursor rendering, mouse/keyboard handling, DoubleTapDetector |\n\n---\n\n## Edge Cases\n\n| Case | Handling |\n|------|----------|\n| Overlapping cursors | Deduplicate after operations |\n| Overlapping selections | Merge into single selection |\n| Selection past EOF | Clamp to document end |\n| Empty document | Allow cursor at (0,0), empty selection |\n| Rectangle on short lines | Clamp column to line length |\n| Undo with multi-cursor | Restore all cursors/selections |\n| Primary cursor deleted | Promote next cursor to primary |\n| All cursors on same position | Keep only one |\n\n---\n\n## Success Criteria\n\n- [ ] Shift+Arrow creates/extends visible selection\n- [ ] Shift+Click extends selection to position\n- [ ] Double-click selects word, triple-click selects line\n- [ ] Cmd+A selects all\n- [ ] Typing with selection replaces selected text\n- [ ] Cmd+Click toggles cursor at position\n- [ ] Middle mouse drag creates rectangle selection\n- [ ] Double-tap Option + Arrow adds cursors\n- [ ] Typing with multiple cursors inserts at all positions\n- [ ] Cut/Copy/Paste works with selections and multi-cursor\n- [ ] Cmd+D selects next occurrence\n- [ ] Undo/Redo restores all cursors and selections\n- [ ] Escape clears selection / collapses to single cursor\n- [ ] All Editor state - cursor, viewport, selections, and view-specific state\n2: \n3: use super::document::Document;\n4: \n5: /// A position in the document (line and column)\n6: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]\n7: pub struct Position {\n8:     /// Line number (0-indexed)\n9:     pub line: usize,\n10:     /// Column number (0-indexed)\n11:     pub column: usize,\n12: }\n13: \n14: impl Position {\n15:     /// Create a new position\n16:     pub const fn new(line: usize, column: usize) -> Self {\n17:         Self { line, column }\n18:     }\n19: }\n20: \n21: /// A text selection with anchor (start) and head (cursor end)\n22: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]\n23: pub struct Selection {\n24:     /// Where the selection started (fixed point)\n25:     pub anchor: Position,\n26:     /// Where the cursor is (moving point)\n27:     pub head: Position,\n28: }\n29: \n30: impl Selection {\n31:     /// Create a new empty selection at a position\n32:     pub fn new(pos: Position) -> Self {\n33:         Self {\n34:             anchor: pos,\n35:             head: pos,\n36:         }\n37:     }\n38: \n39:     /// Create a selection from anchor to head\n40:     pub fn from_anchor_head(anchor: Position, head: Position) -> Self {\n41:         Self { anchor, head }\n42:     }\n43: \n44:     /// Check if selection is empty (cursor without selection)\n45:     pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {\n46:         self.anchor == self.head\n47:     }\n48: \n49:     /// Get the start of the selection (smaller position)\n50:     pub fn start(&self) -> Position {\n51:         if self.anchor <= self.head {\n52:             self.anchor\n53:         } else {\n54:             self.head\n55:         }\n56:     }\n57: \n58:     /// Get the end of the selection (larger position)\n59:     pub fn end(&self) -> Position {\n60:         if self.anchor <= self.head {\n61:             self.head\n62:         } else {\n63:             self.anchor\n64:         }\n65:     }\n66: \n67:     /// Check if the selection is reversed (head before anchor)\n68:     pub fn is_reversed(&self) -> bool {\n69:         self.head < self.anchor\n70:     }\n71: }\n72: \n73: /// Cursor position in the document\n74: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]\n75: pub struct Cursor {\n76:     /// Line number (0-indexed)\n77:     pub line: usize,\n78:     /// Column number (0-indexed)\n79:     pub column: usize,\n80:     /// Desired column for vertical movement (preserves position when moving through short lines)\n81:     pub desired_column: Option<usize>,\n82: }\n83: \n84: impl Cursor {\n85:     /// Create a new cursor at position (0, 0)\n86:     pub fn new() -> Self {\n87:         Self::default()\n88:     }\n89: \n90:     /// Create a cursor at a specific position\n91:     pub fn at(line: usize, column: usize) -> Self {\n92:         Self {\n93:             line,\n94:             column,\n95:             desired_column: None,\n96:         }\n97:     }\n98: \n99:     /// Convert to Position (without desired_column)\n100:     pub fn to_position(&self) -> Position {\n101:         Position::new(self.line, self.column)\n102:     }\n103: \n104:     /// Create from Position\n105:     pub fn from_position(pos: Position) -> Self {\n106:         Self::at(pos.line, pos.column)\n107:     }\n108: \n109:     /// Reset the desired column (called after horizontal movement)\n110:     pub fn clear_desired_column(&mut self) {\n111:         self.desired_column = None;\n112:     }\n113: \n114:     /// Set the desired column (called before vertical movement if not set)\n115:     pub fn remember_column(&mut self) {\n116:         if self.desired_column.is_none() {\n117:             self.desired_column = Some(self.column);\n118:         }\n119:     }\n120: }\n121: \n122: /// Viewport state - what portion of the document is visible\n123: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n124: pub struct Viewport {\n125:     /// First visible line (0-indexed)\n126:     pub top_line: usize,\n127:     /// First visible column (for horizontal scrolling)\n128:     pub left_column: usize,\n129:     /// Number of lines that fit in the viewport\n130:     pub visible_lines: usize,\n131:     /// Number of columns that fit in the viewport\n132:     pub visible_columns: usize,\n133: }\n134: \n135: impl Viewport {\n136:     /// Create a new viewport with the given dimensions\n137:     pub fn new(visible_lines: usize, visible_columns: usize) -> Self {\n138:         Self {\n139:             top_line: 0,\n140:             left_column: 0,\n141:             visible_lines,\n142:             visible_columns,\n143:         }\n144:     }\n145: }\n146: \n147: impl Default for Viewport {\n148:     fn default() -> Self {\n149:         Self::new(25, 80)\n150:     }\n151: }\n152: \n153: /// Editor state - view-specific state for editing a document\n154: ///\n155: /// Supports multiple cursors and selections for future multi-cursor editing.\n156: /// Currently, most operations work on the primary cursor (index 0).\n157: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n158: pub struct EditorState {\n159:     /// All cursors (primary cursor is at index 0)\n160:     pub cursors: Vec<Cursor>,\n161:     /// Selections corresponding to each cursor (parallel to cursors)\n162:     pub selections: Vec<Selection>,\n163:     /// Viewport showing which portion of the document is visible\n164:     pub viewport: Viewport,\n165:     /// Number of lines of padding to maintain above/below cursor when scrolling\n166:     pub scroll_padding: usize,\n167: }\n168: \n169: impl EditorState {\n170:     /// Create a new editor state with default settings\n171:     pub fn new() -> Self {\n172:         let cursor = Cursor::new();\n173:         let selection = Selection::new(cursor.to_position());\n174:         Self {\n175:             cursors: vec![cursor],\n176:             selections: vec![selection],\n177:             viewport: Viewport::default(),\n178:             scroll_padding: 1, // JetBrains-style default\n179:         }\n180:     }\n181: \n182:     /// Create an editor state with specific viewport dimensions\n183:     pub fn with_viewport(visible_lines: usize, visible_columns: usize) -> Self {\n184:         let cursor = Cursor::new();\n185:         let selection = Selection::new(cursor.to_position());\n186:         Self {\n187:             cursors: vec![cursor],\n188:             selections: vec![selection],\n189:             viewport: Viewport::new(visible_lines, visible_columns),\n190:             scroll_padding: 1,\n191:         }\n192:     }\n193: \n194:     /// Get the primary cursor (read-only)\n195:     #[inline]\n196:     pub fn cursor(&self) -> &Cursor {\n197:         &self.cursors[0]\n198:     }\n199: \n200:     /// Get the primary cursor (mutable)\n201:     #[inline]\n202:     pub fn cursor_mut(&mut self) -> &mut Cursor {\n203:         &mut self.cursors[0]\n204:     }\n205: \n206:     /// Get the primary selection (read-only)\n207:     #[inline]\n208:     pub fn selection(&self) -> &Selection {\n209:         &self.selections[0]\n210:     }\n211: \n212:     /// Get the primary selection (mutable)\n213:     #[inline]\n214:     pub fn selection_mut(&mut self) -> &mut Selection {\n215:         &mut self.selections[0]\n216:     }\n217: \n218:     /// Check if there are multiple cursors\n219:     pub fn has_multiple_cursors(&self) -> bool {\n220:         self.cursors.len() > 1\n221:     }\n222: \n223:     /// Get the number of cursors\n224:     pub fn cursor_count(&self) -> usize {\n225:         self.cursors.len()\n226:     }\n227: \n228:     /// Collapse all cursors to just the primary cursor\n229:     pub fn collapse_to_primary(&mut self) {\n230:         self.cursors.truncate(1);\n231:         self.selections.truncate(1);\n232:     }\n233: \n234:     /// Update the primary selection to match the primary cursor (for non-selection moves)\n235:     pub fn clear_selection(&mut self) {\n236:         let pos = self.cursors[0].to_position();\n237:         self.selections[0] = Selection::new(pos);\n238:     }\n239: \n240:     /// Toggle a cursor at the given position\n241:     /// If a cursor exists at that position, remove it (unless it's the only one)\n242:     /// If no cursor exists there, add one\n243:     /// Returns true if a cursor was added, false if removed\n244:     pub fn toggle_cursor_at(&mut self, line: usize, column: usize) -> bool {\n245:         // Check if there's already a cursor at this position\n246:         let existing_idx = self.cursors.iter().position(|c| c.line == line && c.column == column);\n247: \n248:         if let Some(idx) = existing_idx {\n249:             // Cursor exists - remove it if not the only one\n250:             if self.cursors.len() > 1 {\n251:                 self.cursors.remove(idx);\n252:                 self.selections.remove(idx);\n253:                 return false;\n254:             }\n255:             // Can't remove the only cursor\n256:             return false;\n257:         }\n258: \n259:         // No cursor at this position - add one\n260:         let new_cursor = Cursor::at(line, column);\n261:         let new_selection = Selection::new(Position::new(line, column));\n262:         self.cursors.push(new_cursor);\n263:         self.selections.push(new_selection);\n264: \n265:         // Sort cursors by position (line, then column) to maintain order\n266:         self.sort_cursors();\n267: \n268:         true\n269:     }\n270: \n271:     /// Add a cursor at the given position (without toggle behavior)\n272:     pub fn add_cursor_at(&mut self, line: usize, column: usize) {\n273:         // Check if cursor already exists\n274:         let exists = self.cursors.iter().any(|c| c.line == line && c.column == column);\n275:         if exists {\n276:             return;\n277:         }\n278: \n279:         let new_cursor = Cursor::at(line, column);\n280:         let new_selection = Selection::new(Position::new(line, column));\n281:         self.cursors.push(new_cursor);\n282:         self.selections.push(new_selection);\n283: \n284:         self.sort_cursors();\n285:     }\n286: \n287:     /// Sort cursors by position (line, then column)\n288:     fn sort_cursors(&mut self) {\n289:         // Create pairs of (cursor, selection), sort by cursor position, then unzip\n290:         let mut pairs: Vec<_> = self.cursors.iter().cloned()\n291:             .zip(self.selections.iter().cloned())\n292:             .collect();\n293: \n294:         pairs.sort_by(|(a, _), (b, _)| {\n295:             a.line.cmp(&b.line).then_with(|| a.column.cmp(&b.column))\n296:         });\n297: \n298:         self.cursors = pairs.iter().map(|(c, _)| c.clone()).collect();\n299:         self.selections = pairs.iter().map(|(_, s)| s.clone()).collect();\n300:     }\n301: \n302:     /// Update viewport dimensions (e.g., on window resize)\n303:     pub fn resize_viewport(&mut self, visible_lines: usize, visible_columns: usize) {\n304:         self.viewport.visible_lines = visible_lines;\n305:         self.viewport.visible_columns = visible_columns;\n306:     }\n307: \n308:     /// Ensure the primary cursor is visible within the viewport with padding\n309:     pub fn ensure_cursor_visible(&mut self, document: &Document) {\n310:         let cursor = &self.cursors[0];\n311:         let padding = self.scroll_padding;\n312:         let total_lines = document.line_count();\n313: \n314:         // Vertical scrolling\n315:         if total_lines > self.viewport.visible_lines {\n316:             let top_boundary = self.viewport.top_line + padding;\n317:             let bottom_boundary =\n318:                 self.viewport.top_line + self.viewport.visible_lines - padding - 1;\n319: \n320:             // Snap viewport to show cursor with padding\n321:             if cursor.line < top_boundary {\n322:                 self.viewport.top_line = cursor.line.saturating_sub(padding);\n323:             } else if cursor.line > bottom_boundary {\n324:                 let desired_top = cursor.line + padding + 1;\n325:                 self.viewport.top_line = desired_top.saturating_sub(self.viewport.visible_lines);\n326:             }\n327: \n328:             // Clamp to valid range\n329:             let max_top = total_lines.saturating_sub(self.viewport.visible_lines);\n330:             self.viewport.top_line = self.viewport.top_line.min(max_top);\n331:         } else {\n332:             self.viewport.top_line = 0;\n333:         }\n334: \n335:         // Horizontal scrolling (always check, independent of vertical)\n336:         const HORIZONTAL_MARGIN: usize = 4;\n337:         let left_safe = self.viewport.left_column + HORIZONTAL_MARGIN;\n338:         let right_safe =\n339:             self.viewport.left_column + self.viewport.visible_columns - HORIZONTAL_MARGIN;\n340: \n341:         if cursor.column < left_safe {\n342:             // Scroll left: put cursor exactly at left safe boundary\n343:             self.viewport.left_column = cursor.column.saturating_sub(HORIZONTAL_MARGIN);\n344:         } else if cursor.column >= right_safe {\n345:             // Scroll right: put cursor exactly at right safe boundary\n346:             self.viewport.left_column =\n347:                 cursor.column + HORIZONTAL_MARGIN + 1 - self.viewport.visible_columns;\n348:         }\n349:     }\n350: \n351:     /// Set primary cursor position from buffer offset\n352:     pub fn set_cursor_from_offset(&mut self, document: &Document, offset: usize) {\n353:         let (line, column) = document.offset_to_cursor(offset);\n354:         self.cursors[0].line = line;\n355:         self.cursors[0].column = column;\n356:         self.cursors[0].desired_column = None;\n357:         self.clear_selection();\n358:     }\n359: \n360:     /// Get buffer offset from primary cursor position\n361:     pub fn cursor_offset(&self, document: &Document) -> usize {\n362:         document.cursor_to_offset(self.cursors[0].line, self.cursors[0].column)\n363:     }\n364: \n365:     /// Get the length of the current line (based on primary cursor)\n366:     pub fn current_line_length(&self, document: &Document) -> usize {\n367:         document.line_length(self.cursors[0].line)\n368:     }\n369: }\n370: \n371: impl Default for EditorState {\n372:     fn default() -> Self {\n373:         Self::new()\n374:     }\n375: }",

Color {\n10:     pub r: u8,\n11:     pub g: u8,\n12:     pub b: u8,\n13:     pub a: u8,\n14: }\n15: \n16: impl Color {\n17:     /// Create a new color from RGB values (alpha defaults to 255)\n18:     pub const fn rgb(r: u8, g: u8, b: u8) -> Self {\n19:         Self { r, g, b, a: 255 }\n20:     }\n21: \n22:     /// Create a new color from RGBA values\n23:     pub const fn rgba(r: u8, g: u8, b: u8, a: u8) -> Self {\n24:         Self { r, g, b, a }\n25:     }\n26: \n27:     /// Convert to ARGB u32 for softbuffer\n28:     pub fn to_argb_u32(&self) -> u32 {\n29:         ((self.a as u32) << 24) | ((self.r as u32) << 16) | ((self.g as u32) << 8) | (self.b as u32)\n30:     }\n31: \n32:     /// Parse from \"#RRGGBB\" or \"#RRGGBBAA\" hex string\n33:     pub fn from_hex(s: &str) -> Result<Self, String> {\n34:         let s = s.trim_start_matches('#');\n35:         match s.len() {\n36:             6 => Ok(Color {\n37:                 r: u8::from_str_radix(&s[0..2], 16).map_err(|e|          a: 255,\n41:             }),\n42:             8 => Ok(Color {\n43:                 r: u8::from_str_radix(&s[0..2], 16).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,\n44:                 g: ```
1) TL;DR
You’re in a good place: the Elm-style refactor and basic-to-advanced selection/multi‑cursor behavior (including multi-cursor editing and clipboard) are largely implemented and broadly match the design docs. The main gaps are: (1) no rectangle selection / AddCursorAbove/Below / find‑and‑select yet, (2) multi‑cursor undo/redo is still single‑cursor-centric, and (3) the structured status bar and split-view architectures are essentially still at the “design only” stage. Next steps should focus on solidifying invariants and undo/redo for multi‑cursor, then finishing the remaining selection phases, before tackling status bar and split view.

---

2) Recommended approach (simple path, with priorities & effort)

**Priority 1 – Solidify selection & multi-cursor core (M–L)**
Focus: correctness, invariants, and undo/redo now that the features are mostly there.

1. **Enforce selection/cursor invariants centrally**
   - Add the helper methods from the design to `Selection` in `editor.rs`:
     - `extend_to`, `collapse_to_head`, `collapse_to_start`, `collapse_to_end`, `contains`, `get_text`.
   - Replace ad-hoc anchor/head manipulations in `update.rs` with these helpers, at least for the primary selection.
   - Add a debug-only assertion helper on `EditorState` (e.g. `debug_assert!(cursors.len() == selections.len())` and `cursors[i].to_position() == selections[i].head`) that you call at the end of `update_editor` and `update_document`.

2. **Unify “movement with/without selection” semantics**
   - Refactor `move_cursor_*` helpers in `update.rs` to follow the design’s pattern:
     - If `extend_selection == true`, move the head and keep anchor.
     - If no extend and selection is non-empty, collapse to start/end before moving.
     - After any move, call `selection.collapse_to_head()` for the primary selection when selection should be cleared.
   - This reduces subtle divergence from the behavior described in `SELECTION_MULTICURSOR_DESIGN.md` (especially for Left/Right with an active selection).

3. **Multi-cursor undo/redo: introduce `EditOperation::Batch` (even minimally)**
   - In `model/document.rs`:
     - Add the `Batch` variant exactly as in the design:
       ```rust
       Batch {
           operations: Vec<EditOperation>,
           cursors_before: Vec<Cursor>,
           selections_before: Vec<Selection>,
           cursors_after: Vec<Cursor>,
           selections_after: Vec<Selection>,
       }
       ```
   - In `update_document`:
     - For multi-cursor paths (`InsertChar`, `InsertNewline`, `DeleteBackward`, `DeleteForward`, and any others you’ve already made multi-cursor aware):
       - Snapshot `cursors_before` and `selections_before` before editing.
       - Collect the per-cursor `EditOperation::Insert` / `Delete` into a `Vec<EditOperation>`.
       - After modifications, snapshot `cursors_after` and `selections_after`.
       - Push a single `EditOperation::Batch` instead of the current “single edit for undo (simplified)” workaround.
   - In undo/redo:
     - Implement `undo_batch`/`redo_batch` following the design: apply inner operations in reverse/forward order and restore cursors/selections from the batch.
   - This aligns behavior with the design doc’s Part 10 and avoids subtle corruption when undoing multi-cursor edits.

4. **Deduplicate/merge cursors after multi-cursor edits (S–M)**
   - Add a method on `EditorState`:
     ```rust
     pub fn deduplicate_cursors(&mut self) {
         // sort_cursors() first, then collapse identical positions
     }
     ```
   - After any multi-cursor editing pass in `update_document`, call `deduplicate_cursors()`.
   - This addresses the “Overlapping cursors / All cursors on same position” edge cases called out in the design.

---

**Priority 2 – Finish remaining selection/multi-cursor phases (M–L)**

5. **Rectangle selection (Phase 7 in PROGRESS, Part 7 of design)**
   - In `editor.rs`, add a `RectangleSelectionState` to `EditorState` as described:
     - Probably something like:
       ```rust
       pub struct RectangleSelectionState {
           pub start: Position,
           pub end: Position,
           pub active: bool,
       }
       ```
   - Wire `EditorMsg::StartRectangleSelection / UpdateRectangleSelection / FinishRectangleSelection / CancelRectangleSelection` in `update_editor`:
     - On `Start*`: store starting position and mark active.
     - On `Update*`: compute the rectangle (min/max line, min/max column) and update `cursors`/`selections` to one cursor per line within the rectangle, clamped to line length.
     - On `Finish*`: leave the resulting cursors/selections active, clear rectangle state.
     - On `Cancel*`: revert to single cursor + selection or last stable state.
   - Ensure rendering uses the existing selection theming for these generated selections (likely already works if `selections` are rendered generically).

6. **AddCursorAbove/Below (Phase 8)**
   - Implement a simple, non-double-tap version first:
     - For `EditorMsg::AddCursorAbove/Below`, for each existing cursor:
       - Compute the line above/below (clamped to document), preserving column (using your line-length clamping).
       - Use `EditorState::add_cursor_at()` to add cursors at these positions.
     - Call `deduplicate_cursors()` afterwards.
   - Optionally later, add the full `DoubleTapDetector` from the design; for now, simple keybindings keep the feature easy to maintain.

7. **Find & Select (Phase 9)**
   - Implement `SelectNextOccurrence` and `SelectAllOccurrences` in `update_editor` or a dedicated helper:
     - Derive the “needle” from:
       - If selection(s) exist: use primary selection text.
       - Else: word under primary cursor (using `char_type` as you do for `SelectWord`).
     - Use `Document` helpers to search forward/backward.
     - Add new cursors/selections at each occurrence (again using `add_cursor_at` and the selection helpers).
   - Be careful to handle overlapping text and line endings; start with simple forward-search semantics and refine later.

---

**Priority 3 – Structured status bar foundation (M)**

8. **Introduce `StatusBar` and segments into `UiState` without changing visuals yet**
   - In `model/ui.rs`, add:
     - `StatusSegment`, `SegmentId`, `SegmentContent`, `StatusBar` as per `STATUS_BAR_DESIGN.md` (you can initially omit interactivity and MessageStyle).
     - Replace or augment `status_message: String` with `status_bar: StatusBar` and a `StatusBar::default_layout()`.
   - In `messages.rs`, add the new `UiMsg` variants:
     - `UpdateSegment { id, content }`, `SetTransientMessage { .. }`, `ClearTransientMessage`.
   - In `update_ui`, implement the minimal update logic and a `sync_status_bar(model: &mut AppModel)` helper:
     - Update filename, modified indicator, cursor position, line count, and selection count segments.
   - In the renderer, you can continue to render a single concatenated string built from the segments to avoid a big rendering refactor now; just move the data model to the new structure.

---

**Priority 4 – Prepare for split view with minimal disruption (L–XL, but can be staged)**

9. **Wrap existing Document/EditorState in a single-group `EditorArea` (Phase 1 + migration path)**
   - Introduce the core ID types (`DocumentId`, `EditorId`, `GroupId`, `TabId`) and `EditorArea` from `SPLIT_VIEW_DESIGN.md`, but implement only the `EditorArea::single_document(document, editor)` constructor and the minimal `focused_document()` / `focused_editor()` helpers.
   - Replace `AppModel { document, editor, .. }` with `AppModel { editor_area, .. }` and:
     - Replace usages of `model.document` / `model.editor` via these helpers.
   - Defer:
     - Any actual splitting behavior, tab bar rendering, splitter dragging, etc.
   - This sets you up for multi-pane work later without changing behavior today.

---

3) Rationale and trade-offs

- **Why focus first on invariants & undo/redo?**
  Selection and multi-cursor are now complex enough that silent inconsistencies (e.g., cursor/selection mismatch, bad undo with multiple cursors) are the highest risk. Fixing these early is cheaper than chasing subtle bugs later, and it aligns behavior tightly with the design doc’s semantics.
- **Why not fully implement all status bar and split-view features now?**
  Both status bar and split view are significant architecture changes. The current single-pane editor already works; the most leveraged improvements are in making existing multi-cursor features robust. Status bar and split view can then build atop a stable core.
- **Why a simple implementation of AddCursorAbove/Below and rectangle selection first?**
  The double-tap Option behavior and full rectangle UX are polish. A straightforward version gives most of the power for a fraction of the complexity; you can refine UX later if adoption warrants.

---

4) Risks and guardrails

- **Risk: Divergent behavior from design in edge cases.**
  - Guardrail: Add a small suite of property-like tests around selection behavior (e.g., extend then collapse, crossing anchor/head) and multi-cursor edits (same text inserted at all cursors, undo restores all positions).
- **Risk: Invariants broken via public fields.**
  - Guardrail: Either gradually make `EditorState` fields private with accessors or, at least, centralize mutation in a few methods and call the debug invariant checker frequently during development.
- **Risk: Undo/redo regressions when introducing `Batch`.**
  - Guardrail: Keep the existing single-cursor paths exactly as-is and gate multi-cursor batch recording behind `has_multiple_cursors()`. Add explicit tests exercising undo/redo with 2–3 cursors, including overlapping edits.
- **Risk: Status bar complexity bleeding into unrelated code.**
  - Guardrail: Treat `StatusBar` as a self-contained model component; only expose a `sync_status_bar(&mut AppModel)` helper and keep renderer changes minimal initially.

---

5) When to consider the advanced path

Move beyond the “simple path” above if:

- You start seeing **frequent UX complaints or subtle bugs** around selection/multi-cursor (e.g., overlapping cursors, unexpected undo behavior) even after the invariant and `Batch` work.
- You need **full-featured status bar interactivity** (clickable encoding, git status, etc.) for daily use.
- You want **true IDE-like multi-pane workflows** (e.g., pair-programming, reference and definition side-by-side, multi-file layouts) — that’s when fully implementing `EditorArea`/layout tree and split view features becomes worth the investment.

---

6) Optional advanced path (brief outline)

If/when you choose the advanced route:

- **Advanced selection/multi-cursor**
  - Implement full “expand selection” (JetBrains Cmd+W) hierarchy and structural selections.
  - Add per-cursor visual line highlights and integrate selection metrics into the status bar (e.g., `(N chars, M lines)` segment).
  - Support configuration for selection granularity and case sensitivity in find/select.

- **Full structured status bar and theming**
  - Extend `StatusBarTheme` as in the design (per-segment styles, transient message colors).
  - Implement the full `StatusBar::layout()` algorithm with left/center/right alignment and overflow handling.
  - Add mouse hit-testing on status bar segments and `SegmentClicked(SegmentId)` handling.

- **Complete split-view system**
  - Implement the full `LayoutNode`/`SplitContainer` tree and `compute_layout()`.
  - Add tab bar rendering and navigation, split/close commands, and splitter drag.
  - Wire edits through `EditorArea` so all views of a `Document` stay synchronized, including cursor adjustments across views.

---

### Appendix A – Snapshot: implementation vs design (key points)

**1. Selection/multi-cursor vs `SELECTION_MULTICURSOR_DESIGN.md`**

- **Implemented & aligned:**
  - `Position`, `Selection`, `Cursor`, `EditorState { cursors: Vec<Cursor>, selections: Vec<Selection> }` (`editor.rs`): structure matches the design.
  - Theming extensions: `selection_background` and `secondary_cursor_color` in `EditorTheme` (`theme.rs`) as required.
  - Messages: All major selection/multi-cursor messages are present in `EditorMsg` (`messages.rs`):
    - Movement with selection (`MoveCursorWithSelection`, `MoveCursorLineStartWithSelection`, etc.).
    - Selection commands (`SelectAll`, `SelectWord`, `SelectLine`, `ExtendSelectionToPosition`, `ClearSelection`).
    - Multi-cursor (`ToggleCursorAtPosition`, `AddCursorAbove`, `AddCursorBelow`, `CollapseToSingleCursor`, `RemoveCursor`).
    - Find & select, rectangle selection messages exist as variants.
  - Basic selection movement logic (Shift+Arrow, Shift+Home/End) implemented in `update.rs` (e.g., `MoveCursorWithSelection` branch).
  - Selection-aware editing:
    - `delete_selection()` helper exists (snippet shows it) and is used by `InsertChar`, `InsertNewline`, `DeleteBackward`, `DeleteForward`.
  - Multi-cursor editing:
    - `DocumentMsg::InsertChar/InsertNewline/DeleteBackward/DeleteForward` process all cursors in reverse document order via `cursors_in_reverse_order`, updating each cursor and its selection.
  - Clipboard:
    - `DocumentMsg` includes `Copy`, `Cut`, `Paste`; `PROGRESS.md` marks Phase 6 (clipboard) complete.

- **Partially implemented / diverging from design:**
  - `Selection` helpers (`extend_to`, `collapse_to_start`, `collapse_to_end`, `contains`, `get_text`) are described in the design but not present in `editor.rs`; instead, selection manipulation is open-coded in `update.rs`.
  - The invariant `cursors[i].to_position() == selections[i].head` is implied but not enforced or centralized.
  - Movement-with-selection semantics mostly match, but collapse behavior for non-shift movement is handled procedurally in each movement function; it’s easy for them to drift from the spec if new movement paths are added.

- **Not yet implemented (per PROGRESS.md status & code):**
  - Rectangle selection behavior:
    - Messages exist (`StartRectangleSelection`, etc.), but `EditorState` has no rectangle selection state, and `update.rs` doesn’t show an implementation. PROGRESS marks Phase 7 (Rectangle Selection) as pending.
  - AddCursorAbove/Below:
    - Messages exist, but PROGRESS flags Phase 8 as TODO; implementations are presumably missing or partial.
  - Find & Select (Cmd+D, Cmd+Shift+L):
    - Messages exist, PROGRESS Phase 9 is unchecked; logic to scan the document and create cursors/selections by occurrence is not present in the snippets.
  - Multi-cursor-aware undo/redo:
    - Design proposes `EditOperation::Batch`; implementation currently records simplified single operations for multi-cursor edits with comments acknowledging it’s incomplete.

**2. Architecture refactor vs `PROGRESS.md` (Phases 1–6)**

- **Matches design:**
  - Model split into `Document`, `EditorState`, `UiState`, `AppModel` per target architecture.
  - Nested `Msg` enums implemented exactly as described (`Msg::Editor/Document/Ui/App` with `EditorMsg`, `DocumentMsg`, `UiMsg`, `AppMsg`).
  - Async `Cmd` system and file I/O: `SaveFile`/`LoadFile` commands, processed asynchronously (per PROGRESS log).
  - Theming system (`theme.rs`) fully matches Phase 4’s goals; colors are no longer hardcoded.
  - Multi-cursor prep (Phase 5) done; `EditorState` now uses `Vec<Cursor>`/`Vec<Selection>`, with helper accessors.

- **Minor deviations:**
  - The “Target Architecture” in `PROGRESS.md` mentions `ThemeMsg`, `DebugMsg`; current `messages.rs` only defines `Editor/Document/Ui/App`. Perf/debug seems to be handled outside the Msg system (per Phase 6 notes). This is acceptable but slightly diverges from the text diagram.

**3. Status bar vs `STATUS_BAR_DESIGN.md`**

- **Current state:**
  - `UiMsg` only has `SetStatus` and `BlinkCursor`; no segment-based updates.
  - `Theme::StatusBarTheme` only has background/foreground, not the richer per-segment styles.
  - `UiState` (per PROGRESS) still uses a simple `status_message` string.

- **Gap:**
  - None of the core status-bar data structures (`StatusSegment`, `StatusBar`, `SegmentId`, `SegmentContent`) or messages (`UpdateSegment`, `SetTransientMessage`, `SegmentClicked`) are implemented yet.
  - Rendering is still the old “pipe-delimited string” style.

**4. Split view vs `SPLIT_VIEW_DESIGN.md`**

- **Current state:**
  - `AppModel` still has a single `Document` and single `EditorState`; no `EditorArea`, no layout tree, no multiple groups/tabs.
  - `Theme` lacks `tab_bar` and `splitter` sub-themes.

- **Gap:**
  - All the data structures (`DocumentId`, `EditorId`, `GroupId`, `EditorGroup`, `LayoutNode`, `EditorArea`, etc.) are still design-only.
  - No split-related messages or update logic; rendering is single-pane.

**5. Code quality notes**

- Editor state:
  - `EditorState` fields (`cursors`, `selections`, `viewport`) are all `pub`, which makes it easy to violate invariants outside `editor.rs`. Consider slowly tightening encapsulation.
  - `sort_cursors()` clones vectors and then re-collects via `pairs.iter().map(|(c, _)| c.clone())`; this double-clones and can be simplified, but it’s purely micro-optimisation.
- Comments vs behavior:
  - `EditorState` doc comment still says “supports multiple cursors… most operations work on the primary cursor”, but multi-cursor editing is now implemented for several operations; this can confuse future maintainers.
- Undo/redo:
  - The explicit comments “simplified - full undo would need batch” are helpful; they also highlight the main remaining technical debt in multi-cursor support.
